kōṇam - Gr. Gonia, an angle.
kōṭṭai - L. Castrum. E. caster.
mēṟkāṭṭiya teṉ coṟkaḷum, mēlaiyāriyac coṟkaḷum, collaḷavil maṭṭumaṉṟi aṭippakutiyilum oliyum poruḷum ottiruppataik kāṇalām. avaṟṟuḷ mūlam evai, tiripu evai eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭittaṟku, accoṟkaḷellām emmoḻiyil oruṅkē vaḻimuṟait toṭarpuṟṟu oru kuṭumpamāka vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kāṇutal vēṇṭum. avai emmoḻiyil eṅṅaṉam vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟavō ammoḻiyē avaṟṟukkellām mūlam eṉpatu muṭinta muṭipākum. pala iṭaṅkaḷil oru marattiṉ ilaiyum pūvum kāyum kaṉiyumākiya ciṉaikaḷē citaṟik kiṭakku māyiṉ, avai aṇmaiyilō, cēymaiyilō uḷḷa oru marattiṉiṉṟē paṟikkappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. itaṟku māṟāka, acciṉaikaḷiliruntē ammaram vantateṉṟu valippatu uttikkum uṇmaikkum poruntāta iṭumpuk kūṟṟākum. ōr ūriluḷḷa makkaḷ vēṟṟūr ceṉṟu vāḻiṉ, toṉṟu toṭṭu avvūrārē eṉattammaic collikkoḷḷa muṭiyātu. aṅṅaṉam collutaṟkut toṉṟutoṭṭu varum uṟaiyuḷ uṟavu, naṭpu, ayal mutaliyavaṟṟaik kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum. mēṟkāṭṭiya āriyac coṟkaḷ mēlai moḻikaḷil āṅkāṅkē oṉṟiraṇṭākac citaṟik kiṭappaṉavē yaṉṟi, tamiḻiṟpōl maraput toṭarpuṟṟa oru kuṭumpamāka allatu kulamāka vaḻaṅki varuvaṉavalla. āriya moḻikaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ collālum, poruḷālum teṉ coṟkaḷai ottiruppatāl, avaṟṟait taṟceyalāka nērnta oppumaikaḷeṉat taḷḷiviṭavummuṭiyātu. avaṟṟiṉ uṇmait toṭarpaik kāṭṭutaṟku moḻi nūloṭu varalāṟu mutaliya piṟa cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
mēṟkāṭṭiya coṟkaṭkellām mūlam teṉmoḻiyē eṉpatu, piṉvarum coṟṟokutiyāl aṟiyalākum.
kul eṉpatu vaḷaivup poruḷ tarum oru vērccol. ataṉiṉṟu, vaḷaivu kōṇal vaṭṭam vaḷaiyam uruṭci tiraṭci, kuḻaṟci, cuḻaṟci, cuṟṟal cūḻal mutaliya pala uḷaviyaṟkaruttuḷḷa paṟpala coṟkaḷ toṭarntu kiḷaittuḷḷaṉa.
avai varumāṟu :-
kul-kulā-kulavu-kulāvu. kulavutal - vaḷaital.
kul-kur-kura-kuram-vaṭṭamāṉa kutiraikkuḷampu. kural - vaḷainta tiṉaikkatir. kurakam - vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa nīrppaṟavai kuraṅku-vaḷaivu kokki kuraṅkutal-vaḷaital, kuravai-vaṭṭamāka niṉṟāṭuṅ kūttu-kuraṅku. kuṟaṅku-kokki. kuṟaṅku-kaṟaṅku, kaṟaṅkutal-cuḻalutal, kaṟaṅku-kāṟṟāṭi. kaṟaṅkal-vaḷaitaṭi, kū. a, tiripu, oppunōkka, muṭaṅku. maṭaṅku, kuṭumpu-kaṭumpu, kuṟaku-vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa karuvi.
kul-kuṉ-kuṉṉā. kuṉṉāttal-uṭampu kūṉippōtal. kuṉi, kuṉital-vaḷaital, vaṇaṅkutal. kuṉippu-vaḷaintāṭuṅkūttu. kuṉuku, kuṉukutal-kaṭuñ cirippil uṭampu vaḷaital kūṉal-vaḷaivu mutukuvaḷaivu. kūṉi-vaḷaintaciṟṟiṟāl kūṉai-kūṉuḷḷa tōṟcāl.
kul kuḷai-kuḷavi-koṭukkuḷḷa tēṉī. kuḷikai-marunturuṇṭai, vaḷaitaṭi, tiraṭci-kuḻal, kuḻalutal-curuḷtal.
kul kuṇ-kuṇṭu-uruṇṭai, uruṭci, tiraṭci. kuṇṭam-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa paṉṟi, kuṇṭaṉ-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭavaṉ, vaḷaintatu. kuṇṭucaṭṭi-uruṇṭa caṭṭi. kuṇṭā-kuṇṭucaṭṭi, kuṇṭai. uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kāḷai. kuṇṭuccaṭṭi. kuṇṭalam-vaṭṭam, cuṉṉam kuṇṭāṉa kātaṇi.
kuṇ-kuṇa-kuṇaku. kuṇakutal-vaḷaital, kuṇaṅku. kuṇaṅkutal, vaḷaital. kuṇakku-vaḷaivu. kuṇalai-uṭalvaḷaivu, vaḷaintāṭuṅ kūttu kuṇi-muṭamāṉatu. kaṇukku-katalaṇiyum ulōka vaḷaiyam. kuṇukkutal vaḷaittal.
kuḷ-kuṭṭu-muṭṭai (teluṅkuc collāṉa ticaiccol) kuṭam-uruṭciyāṉatu, vaḷaivu, kuṭa-vaḷainta. kuṭavu, kuṭavutal-vaḷaital. kuṭakkam vaḷaivu, kuṭakki-vaḷaivāṉatu. kuṭakkiyaṉ-kūṉaṉ. kuṭaṅku-kuṭaṅkutal-vaḷaital. kuṭantam-vaḷaivu, vaṇakkam, vaḻipāṭu. kuṭantai-vaḷaivu. kuṭā-vaḷaivāṉatu kuṭi-(vaḷainta) puruvam.
kuḷ-kuy-kuyam-vaḷainta arivāḷ.
kuḷ-kūḷ-kūḷi-vaḷainta vāḻaippaḻam.
kūḷ-kūṭu-vaṭṭamāṉa neṟ kaḷañciyam.
kaḷ-koḷ-vaḷainta kāyiluḷḷa karaṇam. koḷ-koṭpu-cuḻaṟci cuṟṟutal. koṭṭu-koṭkutal-cuṟṟutal. (koṭki)-kokki-vaḷainta māṭṭuṟuppu. (koṭku)-kokku-vaḷainta kaḻuttuḷḷa oru nīrppaṟavai vakai. kokkai-kokki. koṭṭu-neṟkūṭu. koṭṭārām-kaḷañciyamuḷḷa nilam.
koṭṭai-uruṇṭai vaṭivam, uruṇṭu tiraṇṭavitai, uruṇṭait talaiyaṇai.
koṭu-vaḷainta. koṭumaram-vil.
koṭukku-vaḷainta muḷḷuṟuppu. koṭukkaṉ-tēḷ.
koṭukki- tēṭkoṭukki (oruceṭi)
koṭi-vaḷainta taṇṭuḷḷa ceṭi
koṭiṟu-kuṟaṭu, kuṟaṭu pōṉṟa vāyalaku.
kul-(kūl)-(kol)-kōl-uruṇṭu tiraṇṭa kompu, kōṟṟoṭi-tiraṇṭa vaḷaiyal, kōli-uruṇṭai.
koḷ-kōḷ-uruṇṭaiyāṉa (allatu cuṟṟivarum) viṇmīṉ. kōḷam-uruṇṭai. kōḷā-uruṇṭaiyāṉa ciṟṟuṇṭi vakai. kōḷam-kōram-vaṭṭil (vaṭṭamāṉa kalam)
kōḷ-kōṇ-kōṇutal-vaḷaital cāytal, kōṇam-cāyvu, cāynta mūlai. kōṇaṉ-kūṉaṉ. kōṇal-cāyvu, neḷivu. kōṇai-cāyvu, piṟaḻvu. kōṇaiyaṉ-matipiṟaḻntavaṉ, kutarukkavāti. kōṇ-kōṭu-vaḷaivu, kōṭutal-vaḷaital. kōṭal-vaḷaivu, vaḷainta itaḻuḷḷa kāntaṭpū. kōṭi-vaḷaivu. kōṭai-kāntaḷ. kōṭṭam-vaḷaivu, matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa kōyil, nilāvaic cūḻnta ūrkōḷ kōṭṭam-kōṭṭakam. kōṭṭai-vaṭṭamāṉa neṟkaḷañciyam, matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa iṭam, nilāvaic cūḻnta oḷivaṭṭam.
ikkuṭumpattaic cērnta piṟa coṟkaḷumuḷa. virivañci immaṭṭil niṟuttap peṟṟatu.
tamiḻppoḻil - tuṇar 32 malar 1
pa-9-18. (1956, ēppiral, mē)
7
vaṭacol teṉcol kāṇumvaḻikaḷ
vēta āriyaruḷ viraliṭṭeṇṇattakka orucilar kimu 2000 pōṉṟa paṇṭaik kālattē tamiḻakattu vantamarntatākat teriyiṉum, kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattilēyē vaṭamoḻiyāl tamiḻ tākkuṇṭa nilaiviḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya tiṉ viḷaivāka vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻip pōrāṭṭam allatu vaṭacol teṉcol vēṟupāṭṭuṇarcci toṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
``iyaṟcol tiricol ticaiccol vaṭacolleṉ
naṉaittē ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē''	(tol.)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (eccaviyal) tolkāppiyar kālattil (ki.mu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) vaṭacol vēṟupaṭuttiyuṇarappaṭṭamaiyai uṇarttumē yaṉṟi tūya tamiḻarāl veṟuttotukkappaṭṭamaiyai - uṇarttātu.
ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakutivarai vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟka ḷellām perumpālum vaṭacollē eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa karuttu, tamiḻa ruḷḷattilum nilaittiruntatu. vaṭamoḻiyilum teṉcoṟkaḷuṇmai kaṇṭu avvuṇmaiyai mutaṉmutal veḷippaṭuttiyavar paṇṭārakar (Dr.) kuṇṭarṭṭu, kālṭuvel, pōppu eṉṉum mēlaiyaṟiñarāvar. ivaruḷ iṭaiñar, talaiciṟantavar. avarum, teṉ collai vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷaik kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi, vaṭacollait teṉcolli ṉiṉṟu pirittuṇarum vaḻikaḷai kūṟiṉarallar. ēṉeṉiṉ atu avaratu tiraviṭa oppiyal ilakkaṇattiṟkut tēvaiyāṉataṉṟu. āyiṉum, avar teṉcoṟkuk kūṟiya vitikaḷait tuṇaikkoṇṭē vaṭacoṟkum cilavaṟṟai vakuttuk koḷḷalām. avai varumāṟu.
vaṭacoṟkaḷ:
1.	mūlavaṭivil āriyaveḻuttum āriya meymmayakkamum amaintiruppaṉa.
2.	paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil vaṭamoḻiyi ṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
3.	tamiḻil vēriṉṟu āriyattilēyē vēr koṇṭiruppaṉa.
4.	tamiḻum tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta tiraviṭamumāṉa ellā moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkātu, avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟālmaṭṭum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
5.	mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum vaḻaṅkuvaṉa.
6.	āriyattiṟ ciṟappup poruḷ allatu ciṟappu viṉait toṭarpu koṇṭiruppaṉa.
cila teṉ coṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉum vaṭa moḻiyiṟ peruvāriyāy vaḻaṅku vatāl perumpāṉmai vaḻakku vaṭacoṉmaikkuc cāṉṟākāteṉa vaṟika.
mēṟkūṟiya vitikaḷ allatu nilaippāṭukaḷ oṉṟiṟku mēṟ poruntiyiruppiṉ, avaṟṟilēmikutikkut takkavāṟu vaṭacoṉmai tēṟṟamām āyiṉum, avai kaṇita muṟaippaṭṭa tullipa vitikaḷalla. ātalāl, parantamoḻi nūṟkalviyum neṭunāṭ collārāycciyum ciṟanta naṭunilaiyumilavēl, avai peritum payaṉpaṭā, uṇmaiyāṉa teṉ collait teṉcolleṉṟu kūṟuvatum naṭuviṉmaiyil etirikaḷ kūṟalām. āyiṉ āḻnta ārāycciyum tūyamaṉac cāṉṟum valiyuṟutti evarukkum eḷḷaḷavum añcavēṇṭuvatiṉṟu cilar aṟivārāycci valimaiyiṉṟit tam paṭṭam patavic ciṟappāl oru muṭipiṟku varukiṉṟaṉar, atu mikat tavaṟām.
paṇṭait tamiḻil vaḻaṅkātu piṟkālat tamiḻil iruvakai vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkum vaṭacoṟkaḷuḷ `pacu' eṉpatu oṉṟākum. itu vaṭacolleṉpatu veḷippaṭaiyāyiṉum, oru cār tamiḻp pērāciriyar itait teṉcol leṉpatāl, māṇavarum ārāycciyillātārum mayaṅvataṟku, iṭaṉā vatuṭaṉ, avaravar virumpiyavāṟē pala vaṭa col teṉcoleṉavum pala teṉ col vaṭacoleṉavum paṭṭu, uṇmaiyāṉa ārāycci valiyaṟṟup pōy viṭukiṉṟatu. pala aṭippaṭait teṉcollai vaṭa colleṉṟu kūṟum vaṭamoḻiyāḷar kūṟṟum valiyuṟṟuviṭukiṉṟatu.
pacuvaik kuṟikkum tūyateṉcoṟkaḷ, ā (āṉ) kaṟavai. kuṭañcuṭṭu, kurāl, kō, curai peṟṟam (irupāṟpotu) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, kaṟavai eṉpatu pāl kaṟakkappaṭuvatu. curai eṉpatu maṭi poruttatu; kuṭañcuṭṭu eṉpatu orukuṭam pālkaṟappatu; kurāl eṉpatu pukar (kapila) niṟattatu. peṟṟam eṉpatu mikap paruttuyarntatu; ā, kō eṉpaṉa potuppeyar. ivaṟṟuḷ ā eṉpatu tamiḻilum tiraviṭattilum maṭṭum vaḻaṅkum; kō eṉpatu āriyattilum vaḻaṅkum.
E. cow, A, sax, cu, G. kub, D. and Dam koe, Ice, ku, Sc, kye, skr. go, gaus.
kōvai mēyppavaṉ allatu vaḷarppavaṉ kōvaṉ, kōvaṉ - kōṉ - kōṉāṉ. oppunōkka ; āyaṉ - āvai vaḷarkkum iṭaiyaṉ
pacu eṉṉum vaṭacol, pācattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum vērp poruḷatu. pācam kayiṟu, āriya vēḷviyiṟ kaṭṭappaṭum uyirikaḷ, māntaṉ, āṉ, kutirai, veḷḷāṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu eṉṉum ainteṉṟu atarvavētam (xi, 2, 9 etc) kūṟuvatāka, māṉiyar villiyam tam vaṭa moḻiyāṅkila vakarātiyiṟ kāṭṭuvar. (pak-611)
pacu eṉpatu mutaṟkālattil, potuvāka, kaṭṭappaṭṭa oru vilaṅkai maṭṭum taṉippaṭavum tokutiyākavum (irupāṟ potup peyarākak) kuṟittu, piṉpu vēḷviyuyirip peyarāka vaḻaṅki, ataṉpiṉ āviṟkuc ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
āriya vaḻakkait taḻuviyē, pacu eṉṉum col tamiḻilum, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkil vilaṅku, kāḷai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu.
``pacuvēṟit tirivāṉōr pavaṉ'' (tēvā. 760, 4) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷaiyaiyum, ``pacukkaḷaip pōlac cellum naṭaiyāl'' (patiṉo. paṭṭiṉat. tiruvēkam. 32) eṉṉumiṭattil vilaṅkaiyum, pacu eṉṉum col kuṟittal kāṇka. āṉ eṉṉum tūyapeṇpāṟ col, tamiḻ marapiṟku māṟāka,
``āṉmukattaṉ aṭaṟkaṇnāyakaṉ''	(kantapu. pāṉukō. 95)
eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāḷaiyaik kuṟippatum āriya vaḻakkait taḻuviyē.
pacu eṉpatu āvai maṭṭuṅ kuṟippatu ulaka vaḻakkākum.
vēta āriyar tamiḻarōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭu civaneṟikkoṇ muṭipu (caivacittāntam) kaṟṟa piṉṉarē, pācam, pacu, pacupati eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, muṟaiyē, taḷai (malam), pulampaṉ (āṉmā). iṟai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷil aṇiyiyaṉ muṟaiyil āḷappeṟṟaṉa.
pacu eṉṉum coṟku iṉamāka, ilattīṉil, Peck (Cattle) bous eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, kirēkkattil bous eṉṉum collum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭaiyum Gaus eṉṉum vaṭa collōṭiṇaippar. mēlai moḻinūlarum akarātiyāḷarum, itu attuṇaip poruttamākat tōṉṟavillai. āyiṉum kakarattiṟkum pakarattiṟkum pōlit toṭarpiruppatāl, atai muṟṟum maṟuttaṟkillai.
Pecu eṉṉum iyaṟ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu paṇattaik kuṟikkum Pecunia (money) eṉṉum ilattiṉ peyarc collum, Pecuniary eṉṉum āṅkilap peyareccamum, celvattaik kuṟikkum māṭu eṉṉum colloṭu oppu nōkkaṟkuriyaṉa.
``kēṭil viḻuccelvam kalvi yoruvaṟku
māṭalla maṟṟai yavai''	(340)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
kō (āṉ) eṉṉum teṉcol mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatum, pecunia eṉṟum ilattīṉ col māṭu eṉṟum teṉcol lottuc celvattai yuṇarttuvatum, pērāciriyar pi.ṭi.cīnivāca aiyaṅkārum, paṇṭārakar irāmaccantira tīṭcitarum kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻaruḷ oru kūṭṭattārē mēlai nāṭu ceṉṟu āriyarāka māṟi yuḷḷamaiyai uṇarttum.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, pacu eṉpatu vaṭacollēyeṉṟum, ataitteṉ colleṉpatāl poyyoṭu kalanta meyyum poyyākip palatūya teṉ collum vaṭacollākat tōṉṟu meṉṟum, ātalāl ataṉāl tīmaiyē yaṉṟi naṉmaiyillai yeṉṟum, pacumpullaittiṉpatu pacuveṉpatu caṟṟum poruntāteṉṟum, aṟintukoḷka.
iṉi, pācam eṉṉum vaṭacol, pacumai, pacai, pāci eṉṉum teṉ coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamiruppiṉum, intiyat tōlāṟ ceyyappaṭum pātakkūṭu (boot) intiyak kālaṇiyākāmai pōṉṟē, teṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirintu oru ciṟappup poruḷai yuṇarttum vaṭamoḻit tiricollum teṉ collākāmai kaṇṭukoḷka.
tamiḻppāvai 4 āṇṭuc ciṟappumalar
(maturai eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam)
8
pāvai eṉṉuñ col varalāṟu
tamiḻiluḷḷa iḷamaippeyarkaḷuḷ pārppu eṉpatu oṉṟākum.
``māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ
pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷaiyum
kaṉṟum piḷḷaiyum makavum maṟiyum eṉ(ṟu)
oṉpatum kuḻaviyō ṭiḷamaip peyarē,''	(1)
``avaṟṟuḷ,
pārppum piḷḷaiyum paṟap pavaṟṟiḷamai,''	(4)
``tavaḻ pavai tāmum avaṟṟō raṉṉa,''	(5)
eṉpaṉa tolkāppiya marapiyal nūṟpākkaḷ.
paṟavaik kuñcum cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ (Reptiles) iḷamaiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉpatu ivaṟṟāl teriyavarum. cila vilaṅkukaḷiṉ kuṭṭiyum pārppeṉappaṭum eṉṟu piṅkala uriccoṟṟokuti kūṟum. itu attuṉaic ciṟappuṭaittaṉṟu, tāyiṉāl mikak kavaṉittup pārkkappaṭu vatiṉāl, paṟavaik kuñcu pārppu eṉappaṭṭatu. pārttal - pēṇutal, pēṇivaḷarttal. pārppu eṉṉuñ col poruṭkaraṇiyam paṟṟi, Nurs(e)ling eṉṟu āṅkilac collai ottatākum. cila ūruyirikaḷiṉ iḷamaikkum pārppu eṉṉum collait tolkāppiyar māṭṭeṟintā rēṉum, atu ciṟappākap paṟavaiyiṉ iḷamaikkē uriyateṉpatai, `ciṟappuṭaip poruḷai muṟpaṭakkiḷatal' eṉṉum utti paṟṟi atai mutaṟkaṇ taṉippaṭak kūṟiyatāṟ peṟavaittār.
pārppu eṉṉum col poruḷ viripu muṟaiyil makkaṭkuḻavi yaiyum kuḻavi pōṉṟa pommaiyaiyum kuṟitta pōtu rakaraṅ keṭṭup pāppu, pāppā eṉat tirintatu.
o.nō: kōr - kō, kōrvai - kōvai.

māviṟkuk kūṟṟamām ñeṇṭiṟkut taṉ pārppu
nāviṟku naṉṟal vacai''	(ciṟupañcamūlam. 9)

tiru eṉṉum col teṉcollā, vaṭacollā?

``nilantarutiruviṟ pāṇṭiyaṉ avaiyattu'' 
eṉṟu tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyirattilum.
``tiruvuṭait tirumaṉaiṉayatu tōṉṟu kamaḻpukai''	(379)
``viṭṭōrai viṭā aḷ tiruvē''	(358)
``tiruḷi lallatu tolaivil laṟiyār''	(20)
eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟilum.

``cērun tiṟaṉaṟin tāṅkē tiru''	(179)
``tiru nutal nallavar nāṇuppiṟa''	(1011)
``naṉṟaṟi vāriṟ kayavar tiruvuṭaiyar''	(1072)
eṉṟu tirukkuṟaḷilum.
``irunilaṅ kaṭanta tirumaṟu mārpiṉ''	(20)
eṉṟu perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyilum
tirunutal (2:41), tiruttakkīr (11:111), tiruñemira kalam (28:157), tirumoḻi (10:12), tirumakaḷ (5:213), tirumāl (17) eṉṟu cilappatikārattilum, iṅṅaṉamē piṟa nūlkaḷilum tiru eṉṉum col palvēṟu poruḷkaḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
ulaka vaḻakkil, tirukkaliyāṇam tirukkārttikai tirukkuṟaḷ tirukkūttu tirunāḷ tirunīṟu tiruppaṉi tiruppati tiruppatikam tiruppāṟ kaṭal tiruppukaḻ tirumakaḷ tirumāṉ tirumaṅkalam, tirumaṅkaliyam tirumaṇam tirumalai tirumāl tirumukam tirumuṭi tirumuṟai tirumuṉ tirumēṉi tiruvaṭi tiruvaraṅkam tiruvaruḷ tiruvaḷḷuvar tiruvākku tiruvācikai tiruvāṭutaṇṭu tiruvāyamalartal tiruviḻā tiruviḷakku tiruviḷaiyāṭal tiruvuḷam tiruvēṅkaṭam tiruvōṭu mutaliya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa coṟkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa.
teyvap peyar aṭiyār peyar teyvattiṉ allatu, teyvat toṭarpuḷḷa ciṉaippeyar āṭaiyaṉip peyar uḷavup peyar viṉaip peyar iṭappeyar nūṟpeyar mutaliyaṉa, tam tūymaiyaik kuṟittaṟkut tiru eṉṉum aṭaipeṟuvatu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkākum.

aṭiyārkaḷ teyvattaṉmaiyuḷḷavarātaliṉ, avarkaḷoṭu toṭarpuṟṟavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷum tiru eṉṟa aṭaipeṟum.
e-kā : tirukkukai, tirukkūṭṭam, tirukkōvai, tirumaṭam, tiruttoṇṭar, tirunāḷ, (tirunaṭcattiram), tiruvēṭam.
tirumakaṉ, tirumakaḷ mutaliya peyarkaḷil tiru eṉṉum aṭaimoḻi pirikka muṭiyātavāṟu piṇaintuḷḷatu.
tiru eṉṉuñ col mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu aṭaimoḻiyāy maṭṭumaṉṟi, taṉic collākavum vantu, muṟaiyē, celvam, ciṟappu aḻaku oḷi ceḻippu pēṟu (pākkiyam) teyvattaṉmai tūymai naṉmai nalviṉai kaṇiyaṉ (cōtiṭaṉ) maṅkalam maṅkiliyam oruvakait talaiyaṇi mārpil tōṉṟum vīṟṟut teyvam mutaliya poruḷkaḷait tarum.
ivaṟṟuḷ, celvam eṉṉum poruḷē mutaltōṉṟiyatum piṟavaṟṟukkellām aṭippaṭaiyumākum.
``poruḷallā tāraip poruḷākac ceyyum
poruḷ tillai poruḷ''
``illāḷai illāṉuma vēṇṭāḷmaṟ ṟīṉṟeṭutta
cellā tavaṉvāyiṟ col.'' 
(tāy vēṇṭāḷ eṉpavaṟṟāl kiṟappum)
`aṉṉamoṭuṅkiṉāl aintu moṭuṅkum' eṉṟum eḷiya iṉattāraik kārokkal (kariya cuṟṟattār) eṉṟum,
``tarittiram mikka vaṉappiṉai yoṭukkic carīrattai yularttā vāṭṭum'' eṉṟum kūṟuvatāl aḻakum oḷiyum;
``tiruvuṭai maṉṉaraikkāṇil tirumālaikkaṇṭēṉē yeṉṉum'' eṉpatāl tūymaiyum celvattāluṇṭātal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē piṟavum.
tiru eṉṉum col, mutalāvatu poruṭ peyarāyp piṉpu paṇpup peyarāyum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. iccolliṉṟu tirumai (kanta. mutaṉāṭ.35) tiruvam (cilap.12) mutaliya paṇpup peyarkaḷum, tiruvaṉ (tiv. iyaṟ. 2, 84)  tiruvāḷaṉ (tiv. periyati. 5, 5, 1) tiruvāṭṭi (tirukkō. 294 urai) mutaliya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa.

tiru eṉṉum `colliṉ vēr'
uyireḻuttukaḷil īkāram allatu ikaram aṉmaic cuṭṭum kīḻmaic cuṭṭum piṉmaic cuṭṭumākum.
aṇmaic cuṭṭai, ivaṉ itu iṅku ivaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷālum; kīḻmaic cuṭṭai, ikam iku iḻi iḷi iṟaṅku mutaliya coṟkaḷālum. piṉmaiccuṭṭai iṭai iṭaṟu iṇuṅku iḻu irai iṟai mutaliya coṟkaḷālum aṟiyalām. (itaṉ virivai eṉatu cuṭṭuviḷakkam eṉṉum cuvaṭiyiṟ kaṇṭu koḷka.)
irai eṉṉuñcollukku, olittal cīṟutal mūccuvāṅkutal vīṅkutal mutaliya viṉaipporuḷkaḷum oli aḵṟiṇaiyuyirikaḷiṉ uṇavu nākappūcci mutaliya peyarp poruḷkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ, mūccuvāṅkutal vīṅkutal uṇavu nākappūcci eṉṉum poruḷkaḷ, iḻuttal eṉṉuṅkaruttai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭaṉa.
mūccuḻuttalukkum, mūcciḻukkum īḷai (kāca) nōykkum iraippu eṉṟum iḻuppuḷḷa mānta nōykku iraippumāntam eṉṟum, iḻuppai yuṇṭākkum elikku iraippeli eṉṟum peyar.
ikarac cuṭṭaṭippiṟappiṉālum rakaraḻakarap pōliyālum iḻuppup poruḷālum irai eṉṉuñcoṟku iṉamāṉatākum. irai eṉṉuñcol mutalil takarameypeṟṟut tirai eṉṟākum. tiraittal iḻuttal. vēṭṭiyai mēl iḻuttuk kaṭṭutalait tiraittuk kaṭṭutal eṉpatu teṉṉāṭṭu vaḻakku. āṭaiyai iḻuttiḻuttu vaittāṟpōṉṟa alaikkum, tōṟcuruṅkaṟkum, orupuṟamākavum kīḻākavum iḻukkappaṭukiṉṟa paṭutāvukkum, tirai yeṉṉum peyariruttalai nōkkuka.

āṭaiyāvatu nīrāvatu tiraiyum pōtu, tiraintaviṭattil tiraṭci yuṇṭāvatāl, tiraitalukkut tiruñatal eṉṉum poruḷum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. pāl tirañatalait tiraitaleṉṟu kūṟuvatai nōkkuka. tiraḷ eṉṉuñ collum tirai eṉpatiṉiṉṟu tirintatē. alaiyāṉatu ōriṭattilillāmal iṭaiviṭātu alaintu koṇṭiruppatāl, alaital viṉai alaiyaik kuṟikkum tirai eṉṉum colliṉ tiripāṉa tiri eṉṉuñ collāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu.
tiru eṉṉuñcol mēṟkūṟiya tirai tiraḷ tiri eṉṉum mūṉṟu coṟkum collālum poruḷālum iṉamākum. tirai pōl ōriṭatti lillāmal eṉṟum alaintukoṇṭiruppatu eṉṉum poruḷilāvatu, makkaḷāl tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruḷilāvatu, celvattiṟkut tiru eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiyirukkalām.
``āṟiṭu mēṭu maṭuvumpō lāñcelvam''
``akaṭuṟayārmāṭṭu nillātu celvañ
cakaṭakkāl pōlvarum''
eṉṟu kūṟiyataṉālum, celvam eṉṉum peyarukkuc celvatu eṉpatu poruḷātalālum, celvam nilaiyaṟṟatu eṉṟum, ākkappaṭuvatāl ākkam eṉṟum tēṭappaṭuvatāl tēṭṭu eṉṟum ceṟintiruppatāl veṟukkai yeṉṟum peyar peṟṟiruppatiṉāl celvam tiraṭṭappaṭuvatu eṉṟum, iru karuttukaḷ nam muṉṉōrkkut tōṉṟiyiruntamai pulaṉām. ivviraṇṭiloṉṟu tiru eṉṉuñ col tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇamā yiruntirukkalām.
tirai eṉṉuñ collē celvattaik kuṟiyāmal, ataṉ tiripāṉa tiru eṉṉuñ col kuṟippatēṉ eṉiṉ, colliṉ poruḷ māṟumpōtu collum māṟavēṇṭum eṉṉum collākkap potuviti paṟṟi yeṉka. allākkāl pala poruḷkaḷ uṭaṉmayaṅkik karuttuṇarvu taṭaippaṭu meṉka.
tirai eṉpatiliruntu tiru eṉpatu tōṉṟiyiruttal tiruppāṟ kaṭaliliruntu tirumakaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ eṉṉuṅ kataiyai nilaiyuṟuttum. tirai = kaṭal. tirai = tirumakaḷ.
``tiraikaṭa lōṭiyun tiraviyan tēṭu''
``nīrpōyum - oṉṟiraṇṭām vāṇikam il''
eṉpavaṟṟālum, paṭṭiṉappālai cilappatikāram maṇimēkalai mutaliya vaṟṟālum, paṇṭaikkālattilum nīr vāṇikattālēyē perum poru ḷīṭṭappaṭṭamai aṟiyappaṭum. kaṭal pōy vanta celvattai kaṭal tanta teṉṟu kūṟutal poruttamāṉatē. mēlum celvattiṉ ciṟanta vakaiyāṉa muttē perumpālum kaṭalilirunteṭukkappaṭuvatu itaṉālum celvam kaṭalil piṟantatākak kūṟalām. celvattiṉ uruvakamē allatu āṭpaṭaiyē (personification) tirumakaḷātaliṉ, avaḷ tirumāliṉ iruppiṭamākak kūṟappaṭum tiruppāṟkaṭaliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiṉatākak kūṟappaṭṭāḷ eṉka. (vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa tiraviyam eṉṉum col tiravam eṉpatiṉiṉṟē tōṉṟiyatu pōlum!)
ākavē, tiru eṉṉuñ col, taṉimoḻiyākavum aṭaimoḻi yākavum mutalāvatu celvapporuḷilum piṉpu ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa pala poruḷkaḷilum toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum taṉit tamiḻc collātal peṟappaṭum. ilattīṉ kirēkku jermāṉiyam mutaliya mēlaiyāriya moḻikaḷil iccollillāmaiyāl, itu vaṭacollaṉṟu eṉpatum veḷiyām. āriyar nāvalantēyattiṟku (intiyāviṟku) vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kalantupōṉa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tirāviṭa allatu teṉcoṟkaḷil tiru eṉpatum oṉṟākum.

tirunāvukkaracu tirumaṅkaiyāḻvār mutaliya peyarkaḷil varum tiru eṉṉum aṭai āṅkilattil aṭiyār peyar muṉ cērkkappaṭum. St. (Saint) eṉpataṟkuc camamāyt tūymai kuṟippatāyiruppatāl, aṭiyārallāta piṟa makkaḷaik kuṟikkumpōtu tiruvāḷar tiruvāṭṭiyār eṉṟa aṭaikaḷaiyē muṟaiyē āṇpāṟkum peṇpāṟkum vaḻaṅkuvatu takkatākum. tiruvāḷaṉmār tiruvāṭṭimār eṉpaṉa palarpāl aṭaikaḷ.
cīkāḻiyaic cīrkāḻiyeṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu cariyāyt tōṉṟavillai. ciṟu taṉam eṉpatu srī taṉam eṉat tavaṟāy vaḻaṅkukiṟatu.
itukāṟuṅkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tiru eṉpatu teṉcollē yeṉṟum ataṉ tiripē srī yeṉṟum terintukoḷka.
10
`uttaram' `takkaṇam' emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
(namatu pāvāṇar avarkaḷ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak kāvalar. tamiḻukkut taṉit tamiḻil akaramutaliyai (ṭikcaṉari) tokuttaḷikkum poṟuppai namatu aracu ivariṭam tāṉ oppaṭaittuḷḷatu. ivaraṉṟi appaṇikku aṟiñar yāruḷar? maṉṟam 1968 il ipperumakaṉāriṉ paṭaippukkaḷai veḷikkoṇara urupā 7500 koṇṭa tokaiyai māṇpumiku kalaiñar karuṇāniti avarkaḷaikkoṇṭu aḷittup perumai koṇṭatu)
tamiḻiṉ colvaḷattai aṟiyātār, vaṭamoḻi kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷaiyum vaṭacoṟkaḷeṉṟē mayaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. attakaiya coṟkaḷuḷ, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām.
tiraviṭaṉiṉ muṉṉōṉāṉa tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamākiya kumarināṭṭil, mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarkaḷ, teṉ, vaṭam, kīḻ, mēl eṉpaṉa. avai 4ām vēṟṟumaikkuriya kuvvuru pēṟṟut teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku (kiḻkku) mēṟku eṉṟāki, piṉṉar āṭci paṟṟi mutal vēṟṟumaiyākavē vaḻakkūṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.
avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum nilaittiṇaic ciṟappum, piṉṉiraṇṭum nilamaṭṭamum paṟṟiyaṉa.
teṉ eṉpatu teṉṉai. teṉṉutal kōṇutal, perumpālun teṉṉik koṇṭiruppatāl teṉṉai appeyar peṟṟatu. teṉ-teṉṉai, teṉ-teṉku-teṟku.
teṉṉai iyaṟkaiyākat tōṉṟiyatu kumarikkaṇṭam. ikkālattil teṉṉaiyiṉ pala vakaikaḷ intō malaiyāviṉ iyaṟkaiyāka vaḷarvatāl annilap pakutiyē teṉṉaiyiṉ piṟantakamāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ appakutiyum kumarik kaṇṭattaic cērntatē yeṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum. paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa eḻu nūṟṟukkāvata nilapparappilirunta palvēṟu nāṭukaḷaik kuṟikkumiṭattu, aṭiyārkku nallār ēḻteṅka nāṭṭaiyē muṟpaṭak kuṟittiruttalāl, annāṭukaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyaṭut tiruntamai peṟappaṭum. teṉkōṭiyil teṉṉai ciṟappāka vaḷarntata ṉālēyē, teṉṟicai appeyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamē nāvalantēyattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyilum vaṭamaram (ālamaram) ciṟantu vaḷarntataṉāl. vaṭaticai appeyar peṟṟatu. vaṭanāvalap pakutiyāṉa vaṅkattil atu ciṟappāka vaḷarvataṉālēyē, mēlaip payir nūlārum ataṟku ``vaṅkāḷa atti'' (Ficushem-gllensis) eṉa ilattīṉap peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ālamaram cūḻa viḻutuviṭṭu vaṭṭamāka akaṉṟu vaḷarvatāl, vaṭam eṉappeyar peṟṟatu. val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam-vaṭam.
ō.nō. paṭṭam-paṭam=tuṇi. māṉiyar vilyamcu camaṟkiruta-āṅkila akara mutaliyum, vaṭa (vata) eṉṉum ālamarap peyarukku vaṭṭam eṉpataiyē poruṭ karaṇiyamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭṭam - va vaṭa (vaṭṭa)
vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutattil, vaṭṭam eṉpatu varutta eṉṟēyirukkummeṉpatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
vaṭṭam (tamiḻ) - vaṭṭa (pirākirutam) - vrutta (camaṟkirutam)
nila maṭṭam iṟṟait tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē kumarik kaṇṭattilum, mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl, avviru ticaikaḷum muṟaiyē mēl kīḻ eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa.
ulakil uyarnta paṉimalai, oru kālattil kaṭaṟkaraiyāy iruntatu. aṉṟu vaṭaticai kaṭalāl tāḻntum, teṉticai kumarimalaiyāl uyarntum, iruntaṉa. piṉṉar paṉimalaiyeḻunta piṉ iruticaiyum camamāyiṉa. ataṉ piṉ kumarimalai mūḻkiviṭavē, vaṭaticaiyuyarntu teṉticai tāḻntum pōyiṉa. ātalāl, vaṭaticai uttaram eṉṉum peyarum teṉticaikkut takkāṇam eṉṉum peyarum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟoṭu ēṉaiyiru ticaikkum, kīḻ mēl eṉṉum paḻaiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum iru peyarkaḷum uṭaṉ tōṉṟiṉa.
kuḷ-kuḻi. kuḷ-kuṇṭu=kuḻi, paḷḷam kuḷ-kuṇ-kuṇam=paḷḷam kuṇṭuṅkuḻiyumāyirukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
kuṭu-kuṭumi=ucci, māṭavucci, talaiyucci uccikkoṇṭai, uccimuṭi, makuṭam, malaiyucci, koṭumuṭi. kuṭu-kuṭam =uyarcci, mēṭu, kuṭanāṭu=uyarnta malai nāṭāṉa cēranāṭu, mēlnāṭu. 
kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyotta, kuṇam kuṭam eṉpaṉavum kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉak kuvvurupēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya ulaka mutaṉmoḻi yātalāl, cēymai aṇmai muṉmai ākiya mūviṭattaiyuñ cuṭṭum ā (a) ī (i) ū (u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭum mutaṉ mutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya cuṭṭolikaḷāka maṭṭumaṉṟi uyirolikaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālēyē, avai tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkil mutalil vaikkappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum āriyamoḻi kaḷiṟpōl maṟaiyātum tiriyātum iyalpākavē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭuvakaiyilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa mūviṭap peyar vakai yilum, tamiḻ (tiraviṭam) āriyattiṟku (camaṟkirutattiṟku) muntiya teṉṟum, ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyoṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiya teṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil āṅkāṅku mikaveṭutturaittuc cellutal kāṇka.
muṉṉilaip peyar: ūṉ - uṉ (orumai). ūm-um (paṉmai)
uvaṉ uvaḷ uvar utu uvai uṅku eṉpaṉa muṉṉilaip poruḷ kaḷaiyum iṭattaiyumē kuṟittalāl, ukara allatu ūkāraccuṭṭu muṉmaic cuṭṭē. muṉmai cēymaikkum aṇmaikkum iṭaippaṭṭa tātalāl, iṭaimaic cuṭṭeṉpatu oru maruṅku poruntum. āyiṉ, cēymaikku varampillaiyātalāl, muṉmai iṭaimaiyākalām. iṭaimai eṉṟum muṉmaiyākātu.
muṉmai aṇmaikkuc caṟṟuc cēymaiyātalāl muṉmaiccuṭṭu, tamiḻiṟciṟupāṉmai ciṟu cēymaiyaiyum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ cēymaiyaiyum uṇarttum.
e-kā :	uṅku = nī (nīṅkaḷ) irukkumiṭattu taṉayaraik kaṇṭirō  vuṅkaṇeṉṉa'' (cētupu 6:39)
utōḷ - utar (inti) = aṅku. muṉmaik karuttiṉiṉṟu muṟcelavuk karuttuttōṉṟum.
e-kā :	uy = muṉcel, muṟceluttu, untu = muṉtaḷ. ukai = muṟceluttu.
nilamaṭṭattiṟkum nīr maṭṭattiṟkum mēṟpaṭṭa muṟcelavu pakkavāṭṭi lum mēḷōkkiyumirukkumātalāl muṉmaiccuṭṭu uyarccik karuttaiyun taḻuvum. e-ṭu: ukkam = kaṭṭit tūkkuṅkayiṟu ukappu = uyarvu. ukaḷtal = kutittal. ukaḷittal = kutittal. uḷakattal = eḻutal. uccam = uyarnilai. ucci=uyariṭam.

uttaram = 1. paṉimalaiyāl uyarnta ticaiyāṉa vaṭakku (tivā)
Uttara, mfn (compar fr. 1. ud; opposed to adbara. upper, higher, superior (e.g. ultare dantas, the upper  teeth), RV; AV, TS: CHUP; Ragh, and c.; northern (because the northern part of India is high), AV; Mn; Susr.; pancat & c.'' eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akara mutali kūṟutal kāṇka.
2. vaṭakkil tōṉṟum ūḻittī (piṅ)
uttara maṭaṅkal = vaṭavaṉal eṉṉum ūḻittī (tivā).
kumarināṭṭuk kālattil tamiḻar (neytal nilapparavar) kaṟṟuk kaṭalōṭikaḷāyiruntataṉāl, vaṭamuṉaiyil avvappōtu tōṉṟum oru palvaṇṇavoḷiyaik kaṇṭu, ataṟku allatu vaṭavaṉal eṉṟu peyariṭṭaṉar; ataiyē ūḻiyiṟutiyil ulakaiyaḻikkum tīyākavum karutiṉar.
``vaṭavā vaṉalu maṭanaṭaip piṭiyum
arumaip peyaruṅ kutiraiyum vaṭalai''(piṅ. 988)
``vaṭavaik kaṉalaip piḻinteṭuttu'' (antakak. taṉip) 
``veḷḷat tiṭaivāḻ vaṭavaṉalai'' (kamparā. tailamā. 86)
``kaṭukiya vaṭavaṉalait tiṉaivaittatu'' 
aṉal - aṉalam (kaliṅ-402)
``akkaṭaliṉ mītuvaṭa vaṉalniṟkavillaiyō'' (tāyu, paripūr .9) 
mēlaiyar vaṭavaṉalai, Aurora (down) Borealies (northern) eṉṟu āṅkilattilum, vaḻaṅkuvatu muṟṟum poruḷottiruttal kāṇka.
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṭavai eṉṉum teṉcollai, vaṭavā, vaṭavā mukam, vaṭavāmukākkiṉi, paṭapā, paṭapāmukam, paṭapā mukākkiṉi eṉṟu tirittum virittum, peṭṭaikkutirai mukattil tōṉṟiya neruppeṉṟu, poruntāp poyttalākap poruḷ kūṟuvar. atait tamiḻar nampiya piṉṉarē ``vaṭavaik kaṉaṉmāvum'' (takkayākap 694) eṉṟu oṭṭakkūttarum pāṭanērntatu. itaṉāl, tamiḻccol tirikkappaṭṭatoṭu tamiḻar matiyum tirikkappaṭṭamai kāṇka.
j¡F=jhœî bj., ka. takku (taggu) avarkkut takkut toṇṭai, avar takkilē pāṭukiṟār. eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka.
takku - takkaṇam = nilamaṭṭattil tāḻnta teṉṟicai, teṉṉāṭu (decan)
takkaṇam - va. taṣiṇam
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, taṣiṇam eṉṉum collait teṟku eṉṉum poruḷil, taṣiṇākkiṉi, takṣiṇāyaṉam, takṣiṇācalam (potiyam), takṣiṇa pāñcālam, takṣiṇāmūrtti eṉa āḷuvar āyiṉ, takṣiṇam eṉṉum colliṟkut takṣ eṉpatai mūlamākak koṇṭu ataṟkuttiṟamai yāyirattal allatu valamaiyōṭiruttal eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kiḻakku nōkkumpōtu teṉṟicai valappuṟamum vaṭaticai iṭappuṟamum iruppatāl takṣiṇam eṉṉum coṟkuvalam eṉṟum uttaram eṉṉum coṟku iṭam eṉṟum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
takṣiṇam pari = valam vā, valañcuṟṟu.
pratakṣiṇa = valam varukai, valar cuṟṟukai.
takutiyeṉṉuñ coṟku vallamaiyeṉṟum poruḷiruppatāl takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacol taku eṉṉunteṉ colliṉ tiripākavumirukkalām.
o. nō. L. dexter = of or on the right-hand side. 
E. dexterous = deft, adroit, clever, using right hand by preference
E; ME. deft = dexterous skilful.
E. daft. ME. daffte = OE ged & fte (Goth gadaban to be fit) orig. Sense, fitting, suitable.
takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkum, (dextes) eṉṉum ilattīṉac coṟkum, mūlam etuvāyiṉum, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉṉum iru ticaiccollum teṉcollēyeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷāvaṉa:-
1. uttaram takkaṇam eṉpaṉavaṟṟoṭu cērntu vaḻaṅkappeṟum kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum ticaippeyarkaḷ, tūya teṉcol. avai vaṭamoḻiyil illai. avaṟṟōṭu cērnta ēṉaiyiraṇṭum teṉcollākavēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. uyarntōṉ tāḻntōṉ eṉpaṉa pōla, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iraṇṭum oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu etirāṉa uṟaviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ (Relative terms).
camaṟkirutattil, uttaram eṉpataṟku nilavuyarcciyaiyum takṣiṇam eṉpataṟku valappuṟattaiyum, karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
3. uttaram eṉṉum colliṟku aṭiyāṉa ukarac cuṭṭum, takkaṇam eṉṉum colliṟku mūlamāṉa takku eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyum tūya tamiḻ.
4. kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyatu.
5. uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum nāṭṭuppeyarkaḷ allatu ticaippeyarkaḷ, tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarum toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻum. nāvalan tēyattilaṉṟi, vēṟevvāriya nāṭṭilum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkiyatillai.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭiṉṟik katiravaṉ vaṇakkattaiyē ciṟappākakkoṇṭa vētaāriyar, intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉṉarē, kālaik katirava vaṇakkattiṉ pōtu takkaṇam valappuṟamum uttaram iṭappuṟamum iruttal kaṇṭu, avviruticaip peyarkaṭkum muṟaiyē valam iṭam eṉap poruḷ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
``kiḻakku'' mutaliya muṉṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarum ulaka vaḻakkil āḻa ūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, `kuṇakku' (kuṇam) mutaliya piṟṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakkilēyē iṭampeṟalāyiṉa.
ulaka vaḻakkiliṉmaiyālum poyyuṭai vaṭavar col vaṉmai yiṉālumē, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷum vaṭacol leṉum mayakkattiṟku iṭamāyiṉaveṉa, ikkaṭṭuraiyiṉiṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
mutaltaram, kaṭaittaram, mēltaram, kīḻttaram, naṭuttaram, iṭaittaram, ciṟutaram, peruntaram, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟatē uttaram (uyarnta nilai) eṉṉum vaḻakkum puṉka vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvaṭivai uṟaḻtaram (comparative degree) ākkik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ut+tara (led + tara) eṉṟu pakuppar. atu kaṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
muraṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram uttaram eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirut talaiyum nōkkuka.
11
`mati' viḷakkam
colliyal (Etymology) moḻi nūluḷ (Philology) aṭaṅkumēṉum, pala coṟkaḷ pala moḻikaṭkup potuvāyiruttalāṉum, oru moḻic col vevvēṟu vakaiyil piṟa moḻic ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalāṉum, moḻiyārāycci ceyyātār collārāycciyil iṟaṅkutal takātu. moḻiyārāyccikku moḻi nūlilakkiyak kalvi maṭṭumaṉṟi palvēṟu moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa aṟivum coṟṟokutiyaṟivum vēṇṭum.
poruṭpāṭu nōkkic coṟkaḷai,
(1) oru poruṭ pala col (Synonyms)
(2) pala poruḷoru col (Homonyms)
eṉa iru vakaiyākap pakuttaṉar paṇṭait tamiḻ ilakkaṇiyar. ivaṟṟai, muṟaiyē, oru poruṭcol, pala poruṭ col eṉak kuṟukkiyum vaḻaṅka lām. oru poruṭ col eṉpatu orēyoru poruḷuḷḷa col eṉṟum poruḷ paṭiṉum oru poruṭ palacol eṉpatu orumaikku ēṟkāmai yāṉum, iraṭṭuṟaṟ coṟkaḷ pala iṭam nōkkip poruḷ vēṟupā ṭuṇarttalāṉum, oru poruṭcol eṉṉum kuṟiyīṭṭiṟku iraṭṭuṟaṉmai iḻukkākāteṉka.
iṉi, pala poruṭ collum,
(1) oru pērp pala poruṭcol (Homonymy) 
(2) pala pērp pala poruṭcol (Polysemy)
eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. kaṭu eṉṉum col, mikuti, vaṉmai, vali, viraivu, kāram, koṭumai mutaliya pala poruḷai orē pērp poruḷaṭiyil uṇarttutalāl oru vērp pala poruṭ collum; māṇṭāṉ eṉṉum col, māṇ, māḷ eṉṉum iru vēṟu viṉaip pakutikaḷiṉ paṭarkkai āṇpāl iṟantakāla muṟṟātaliṉ pala vērp pala poruṭ collum; ākum.
oru poruṭ collum iṅṅaṉamē oru vēruṭaimaiyum pala vēruṭaimaiyumpaṟṟi iru tiṟappaṭum. āyiṉ, atu ikkaṭṭuraikkup payaṉpaṭāmaiyiṉ viḷakkappaṭṭilatu.
mati eṉṉum col, paruttal, kaṭaital, koḻuttal, mataṅkoḷḷutal, mayakkam, kaḷ, yāṉai, nilā, iṉimai, karuvāppaṭṭai, aḷavu, matippu, aṟivu, acaiccol mutaliya poruḷkaḷil oru vēlai pala poruṭ collāṉa teṉ collum; peṇpāl aṭai moḻiyīṟākavariṉ, `māṉ' eṉṉum tam īṟṟiṉiṉṟu tirinta vaṭa collum ākum. ivviraṇṭiṟkum evvakai toṭarpumillai. ivaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu:-
muttu-mattu matti-mati. u (aḷḷait tiripu),
muttutal=cērtal. (cīva 504), mukattoṭu mukam cērtal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu kūṟukaḷ oṉṟu cēriṉ tiraṭciyuṇṭākumata lāl, cērtaṟ karuttil tiraṭci karuttut tōṉṟum.
``cērē tiraṭci'' (tol. col 363).
muttu-muttai=tiraṭci (cūṭā cōṟṟuruṇṭai).
muttai - mottai=paruma uruṇṭai.
mottai - mōttai=parutta veḷḷāṭṭukkaṭā.
muttu - mottu - mottam tiraṭci, muḻumai.
muttu=tiraṇṭa vitai, mala ḷiñcilil viḷaiyuṅ kal, koppaḷam.
muttu-muttam=periya muttu, iccol vaṭamoḻiyil muktayeṉṟu tiriyum. `am' perumaip poruḷ īṟu.
muttu-mattu=tiraṇṭa tayir mutaliyaṉa kaṭai karuvi. mattu-mattam āymakaḷ mattam piṇitta kayiṟu'' (kalit. 110)
mattu-matti. mattittal=kaṭaiyil.
matti-mati. matittal=kaṭaital, viralāl allatu kaiyāl nacukkip pacaipōlākkutal.
mati-maci. macital=pacaipōlātal (ta. vi.) macittal = pacai pōlākku tal (pi.vi.)
mattu, mattam eṉpaṉa manta (Mantha) eṉṟum; matti, mati eṉpaṉa mat (Math) eṉṟum, maci eṉpatu maṣ eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
mattam = erumaikkaṭā. iccol vaṭamoḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum. potuvāka, ōr iyaṅkutiṇai uyiriyiṉ uṭampu paruttalāl koḻuttalum mataṅkoḷḷutalum uṇṭākum.
matittal=	1. paruttal, upputal.
	2. koḻuttal, cerukkutal.
	'' matittetir tevvā'' (iraku. mīṭci. 37).
3. mataṅkoḷḷutal.
'' matitta kaḷiṟṟiṉiṉ'' (kamparā. pañcakē. 56)
matayāṉai. ``kōṇ matittiṭar kiṭartaṉa'', (kamparā. nākapāca 136)
mati- matam = mikuti, perumai, valimai, vaḷam, cerukku, matanīr.
matam - mataṉ = mikuti, valimai, cerukku.
mattam = matam. mattamā = yāṉai. mattam - matam eṉṟumām.
mattaṉ = koḻuttavaṉ. ``mattaṉirāvaṇaṉ kotittāṉ'' (irāmanā. uyut. 44).
mattam, mattaṉ eṉpaṉa matta eṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
matam-matar. matarttal=mikutal, ceḻittal, koḻuttal, mataṅ koḷḷutal, cerukkutal.
pala poruḷkaḷ allatu vaḻikaḷ allatu karuttukkaḷ kalattalāl, kalakkamum mayakkamum uṇṭākum.
mattittal=kalattal, maruntu kalattal.
mattam = mayakkam. mattam-matam-mataṉ=kalakkam.
o.nō:	kula-kulavu-kalavu, kalavutal = kalattal. kulavutal=kūṭutal. kula-kala-kalaṅku-kalakku-kalakkam. muya-muyaṅku-muyakkam. muyaṅkutal=taḻuvutal, kalattal. muya-maya-mayaṅku-mayakku-mayakkam=kūṭṭam, kalakkam. mayattal=mayaṅkutal. ``mayantuḷē ṉulaka vāḻkkaiyai'' (aruṭpā, apayattiṟaṉ. 14).
mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu, uṇarviṉmai (mūrccai), veṟi, kōṭṭi (paittiyam), kāmam ākiyakaruttukkaḷ tōṉṟum. 
mattam=mayakkam, kuṭi veṟi, kōṭṭi, ūmattai.
mattam - mattaṉ = matimayaṅkiyavaṉ, kōṭṭipiṭittavaṉ. mattam-matam=kuṭiveṟi, kōṭṭi.
mattu=ūmattai. matattal=mayaṅkutal.
mattu, mattam, mattaṉ eṉṉum immuccollum vaṭa moḻiyil matta eṉat tiriyum; mata eṉpatu Mada eṉat tiriyum.
cerukku, yāṉai matam ākiya iru karuttum mayakkak karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākavuṅ koḷḷalām. ō-nō: kaḷi-kaḷiṟu. kaḷittal=kaḷḷuṇṭu veṟittal, mayaṅkutal.
nila voḷiyāl kōṭṭiyuṇṭākumeṉṟu paṇṭaik kālattil kīḻnāṭṭilum mēl nāṭṭilum oru karuttiruntatāl, tiṅkaḷukku mayakkan taruvatu eṉṉum poruḷpaṟṟi oru peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟu.
mattu-(matti)-mati: allatu matam-mati=tiṅkaḷ.
Luna (Latin) = tiṅkaḷ, Lunacy (Eng.) = kōṭṭi.
mati=mātam (matiyāl aḷakkappaṭum kāla aḷavu). 
mati-mātam-māsa (va).
matip peyariṉiṉṟē mātap peyar uṇṭākum. tiṅkaḷ=mati, mātam. (Moon-month). mati eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil tiṅkaṭ poruḷ illai. iyalpākat takaram cakaramākat tiriyumēyaṉṟic cakaram takaramākat tiriyātu. ātalāl, mātam eṉpatu teṉ collātal teḷivu. āyiṉum, āriya mayakkāl tamiḻar pētuṟuvatu avar pētaimaiyē. ēṟkeṉavē tiṅkaṭ col matiyaiyum oru kiḻamaiyaiyum kuṟittalāl, atai mēlumoru poruḷil vaḻaṅki mayakkutal vaḻuvākum. mayakkan tarum kaḷ allatu tēṉ iṉittalāl, mayakkak karuttil matuk karuttum tōṉṟum.
mattu-maṭṭu=tēṉ, kaḷ.
mattu-matu-matur-maturi-maturam.
matu-matam=tēṉ. ``mataṅkamaḻ kōtai'' (cīvaka. 2584).
matu=tēṉ, kaḷ, iṉimai, atimaturam.
matu-mati=atimaturam.
maṭṭu eṉpatu Madya eṉṟum, matu eṉpatu Madhu eṉṟum vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
iṉi, muttu eṉpataṉ valvaṭivāṉa muṭṭu eṉṉum col, oru poruḷ iṉṉoru poruḷai muṭṭutalāl aṟiyappaṭum iyakka muṭivai allatu vaḻi ellaiyai uṇarttutalāl, cērtal allatu poruntutaṟ karuttil ellai allatu aḷavukkaruttum tōṉṟum. mukattāṟ poruntutal muttutalum talaiyāṟ poruntutal muṭṭutalum ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉ, meṉmai vaṉmai kuṟikka, muṟaiyē takara ṭakaram vantaṉaveṉa aṟika. iṅṅaṉamē muṭṭiyāl mutukil aṟaital kuttutalum maṇṭaiyil aṟaital kuṭṭutalum ātal kāṇka. āyiṉum, mutaṟkālattil takarac collē ṭakaraccoṟ poruḷaiyuṅ kuṟittatu. 
muṭṭutal=muṭital.
muṭṭu-maṭṭu=muṭivu, ellai, aḷavu maṭṭu-maṭṭam.
(mattu)-(matti)-mati.
matittal=aḷaviṭutal, uḷḷattāl aḷaviṭutal, uyarvākak karututal, karuttākak koḷḷutal. 
matippu = ēṟattāḻak kaṇippu (kaṇicam), uyarvu.
mati=matippu, aḷaviṭappaṭṭa paṇṭam (ēṟṟumati, iṟakkumati), aḷantaṟiyum pakuttaṟivu.
aṟivuppulaṉ. mati - matam = koḷkai, camayam, nūṟkoḷkai. mati eṉṉum col muṉṉilai paṭarkkai yacainilaiyāy varuṅkāl, aḷavu eṉṉuṅ karuttil `atu pōtum' eṉṟēṉum, aṟivu eṉṉum karuttil `atu aṟivākum' eṉṟēṉum poruḷpaṭum. paḻañcēra nāṭākiya kēraḷattil, mati eṉpatu `pōtum' eṉṉum poruḷil iṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
matam (camayam) eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil Mata eṉa vaḻaṅkum. aḷavīṭu eṉṉum karuttoḻittu, itukāṟuṅ kūṟiya ellāp poruḷkaḷilum, mati eṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṭaiya mata, matam, mataṉ, matar mutaliya coṟkaḷ - tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. muḻukip pōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattoṭu pala ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa paṇṭaiyilakkiyattoṭu pala ceyyuḷ vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, maṟaintu pōṉamaiyāl, pala coṟ kōvaikaḷiṉ iṭaiyilirunta aṇṭukaḷai iṉṟu kāṭṭa muṭiyavillai. āyiṉum, tamiḻ kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaṭakkē ceṉṟu muṉṉart tiraviṭamākavum piṉṉar āriyamākavum māṟiṟṟeṉṟum, vaṭamoḻi āyirakkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷaik kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷateṉṟum, aṟiya vallārkku nāṉ kūṟiya yāvum teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal tēṟṟam.
moḻiyākavum īṟākavum varuṅkāmāṉ eṉṟu maruvum. 
e-kā : marumakaṉ marumāṉ, perumakaṉ - perumāṉ iṅṅaṉamē tirumakaṉ eṉpatu māṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
tiru eṉpatai vaṭamoḻiyar srī eṉṟu tirittukkoṇṭatāl tirumāṉ eṉpatu srīmāṉ eṉa tirintatu itaṉaic cīmāṉ ``eḻuttoṭu puṇarnta collākkiṉar tamiḻar. tiruvaraṅkam-srīraṅkam-cīraṅkam eṉṉum tirivai nōkkuka.
cīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl `cīmāṭṭi'yātalāl, srīmāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl srīmāṭṭi'yum tirumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl `tirumāṭṭi'yum ātal vēṇṭum. tiruvāṭṭi eṉpatu tiruvāḷar eṉpataṉ peṇpāl. marumakaḷ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl marumakaḷ eṉṟum perumāṉ eṉpataṉ peṇpāl perumāṭṭi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka. ``kalai marumāṭṭi'' (tiruviḷai, tirumaṉa 62). ``perumāṭṭi'' (tivā) perumāḷ-pirāṉ perumāṭṭi-pirāṭṭi ivai marūuc citaivu.
`vāṉ' eṉṉum īṟṟiṉil `vat', `vatī' eṉṟu muṟaiyē āṇ pāṟkum peṇpāṟkum tirittuk koṇṭatupōṉṟē, `māṉ' eṉṉum īṟṟiṉiṉṟum `mat', `matī' eṉṟu tirittukkoṇṭaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. ātalāl, peṇpāl peyarkkut tirumati eṉṟu aṭai koṭukkātu tirumāṭṭi eṉṟu koṭuttal vēṇṭum. srī srīmatī eṉpaṉa vaṭa col vaṭivukaḷ. srīmatī eṉpatu eḻutte puṇarnta collākumpōtu ti eṉṟākum. ivvaṭivai vilaki vēṇṭum.
12
`uvamai' teṉcollē
āṅkilak kalviyum aṟiviyal ārāycciyum nam nāṭṭil pukuntu oṉṟarai nūṟṟāṇṭum. tamiḻ taṉi vaḷamoḻiyeṉak kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇi yār nāṭṭi oru nūṟṟāṇṭum ākiyum iṉṟum tamiḻiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyaip paṟṟip pala pērāciriyarum aiyuṟṟu varuvatāl ovvoru tamiḻc collaiyum, vērum ākkamum kāṭṭi iṉṉiṉṉatu teṉ collē yeṉa nāṭṭa vēṇṭum nilaimai nērntuḷḷatu.
kaṇṇutaṟ peruṅ kaṭavuḷuṅ
kaḻakamō ṭamarntu
paṇṇuṟat terintāyntaip
pacuntamiḻ ēṉai
maṇṇiṭaic cila ilakkaṇa
varampilā moḻipōl
eṇṇiṭaip paṭak kiṭantatā
eṇṇavum paṭumō!
eṉṟu parañcōti muṉivar pāṭumāṟu tamiḻilakkaṇam, taṉip paṭṭatāyum piṟa moḻikaṭkillāta poruḷilak kaṇattait taṉṉakattuk koṇṭu talaimai vāyntatāyum ki. mu. pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē kumari nāṭṭil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta toṉmaiyuḷḷatāyum, iruntum ataṉ kuṟiyīṭukaḷil oṉṟākavum poruṭpaṭala iyaṟpeyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟākavum uḷḷa ``uvamai'' eṉṉum col vaṭa collā teṉ collā eṉṉum aiyuṟaviṟkum vaṭacollē eṉṉum valippiṟkum collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum mikka ikkālattu iṭantantu niṟpatu mika mika iraṅkat takka ceytiyākum.
uvamai yeṉṉuñ col oppumai yeṉṉuñ poruḷatu. ip poruḷ ataṉ muḻuvaṭivil maṭṭumiṉṟi ataṉ aṭivērāṉa ukaratti lēyē karukkoṇṭuḷḷatu. ukaramākiya ōreḻut torumoḻi oppumaik karuttuk koṇṭiruppatai uttiyeṉṉuñ collāl uṇaralām uttal poruntutal utti poruttam matikkum aṟiviṟkum poruttamāṉa ceyti utti eṉappaṭum. avvuttiyai aṟiyum akakkaraṇattaiyum uttiyeṉpatu ākupeyar. nūliṟkum uraikkum payaṉpaṭum uttikaḷāṉa neṟimuṟaikaḷ muppattiraṇṭākat tokukkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivvuttiyai yukti eṉat tirittāḷvar vaṭanūlār.
iru kaṭciyar viḷaiyāṭum ellā viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷilum kaṭci yamaittaṟku ivviruvarāyp porunti varuvatu utti kaṭṭutal eṉpar. kaṭṭutal eṉṉum col vaḻakkē uttiyeṉṉum coṟkup porutta poruḷuṇmaiyai uṇarttap pōtiya cāṉṟākum. kaṭṭutal poruttutal, kaḷḷutal, poruntutal kaḷḷa eṉpatu porunta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum ōr uvama urupu ``kaḷḷak kaṭuppa āṅkavai eṇucu'' eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟutal kāṇka. (1232) `kaḷ' eṉṉum pakutiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatē kaḷam (kūṭṭam, avai) eṉṉum tokutip peyarum kaṭci (kaḷ + ci) eṉṉum peyarum ippakutiyatē.
ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akarāti viḷaiyāṭṭu - ttic collait teluṅkuc colleṉṟu piṟaḻak koṇṭatōṭu, nūluttic colliṉiṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa teṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷatu. utti yeṉpataṉoṭu toṭarpuṟṟa untu eṉṉum col poruntutaṟ poruḷpaṭuvatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
``tāṉan tōṟum untiṭuṅkaraṇam'' eṉpatu civañāṉa cittiyār. (4, 34)
tamiḻil ēṟattāḻa mukkāṟ pakuti ukaravaṭivac coṟkaḷ. ukaravaṭi collākkattil
(1) muṉṉait tiripu
(2) aḷḷait tiripu
(3) piṉṉait tiripu
eṉa mūvakait tiripaṭaiyum ukaram ūkāra okara ōkāraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟāvatu muṉṉait tiripu; ukaram akaramāvatu aḷḷait tiripu; ukaram ikaramāvatu piṉṉait tiripu; muṉṉait tiripu eṉṟum mōṉaiyākavē yirukkum.
e-kā :
puḻai	-	pūḻai
kuṭṭu	-	koṭṭu	}	muṉṉait tiripu
muṭaṅku	-	maṭaṅku		aḷḷait tiripu
puraḷ	-	piṟaḻ	}	piṉṉait tiripu
ivaṟṟuḷ piṉṉiraṇṭum mōṉaiyākavum toṭarum aṉṟu, aḷḷai, mōṉai, piṉṉai mōṉai eṉappaṭum.
e-kā :	kuḷiyam	-	kūḷi, koḷ
	kōḷ	-	muṉṉai mōṉai
	pacumai	-	pāci
	paimmai	-	aḷḷai mōṉai
	civaṉ ceyyāṉ
	cēyōṉ	-	piṉṉai mōṉai
kuḷiyam - uruṇṭai, kuḻiyam - vaḷaitaṭi, kūḷi - vaḷaintatāyuḷḷa vāḻai vakai, koḷ - vaḷainta kāyuḷḷa kāṇam, koṭkutal - vaḷaital, cuṟṟutal cuḻalutal, kōḷ - vaṭṭam - katiravaṉaic cuṟṟum kirakam, kōḷ, kōṇ - kōṭu, kuḷ - kūḷ.
ukaraccol mutalāvatu muṉmaik karuttaiyum, piṉpu ataṉ vaḻiyākap piṉmai uyarcci oppumai ākiya karuttukaḷaiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu.
oppumaik karuttil ukarac col u - o - ō eṉa mōṉait tiripaṭai yum. ottal pōtal, ōviyam oppumai paṟṟiya varaivukkalai ō + tal = ottal. ō + iyam = ōviyam okaravaṭiva pala meykaḷoṭuñ cērntu pala oppumai allatu poruntaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷai uṇṭupaṇṇum. okkal, occai, oṭṭu, oṇṭu, oṇṇu, ottu, oppu, ompu, ollu, ovvu, oṟṟu, oṉṟu, oṉṉu ākiya coṟkaḷai nōkkuka.
moḻi vaḷarcciyil, coṟkaḷ palkutaṟ poruṭṭu aṟuvakaic ceyyuḷ tiripukaḷum mūvakaip puṇarcci vēṟupāṭukaḷum mukkuṟaikaḷum, muccērkkaikaḷum muṉṉōrāl kaiyāḷap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. muccērkkai yāvaṉa. mutaṟ cērkkai iṭaic cērkkai, kaṭaic cērkkai eṉpaṉa.
oppumaik karuttuḷḷa ukarac col kaṭaic cērkkaiyāka lakaramey peṟṟup palvēṟu tiripaṭaintu paṟpala coṟkaḷaip piṟappikkum.
ul - uḷ uṭu - uṭaṉ
uṭu - oṭu - ōṭu
ōṭutal - ottal, poruntutal kūṭutal. ōṭa eṉpatu ōr uvama urupu.
``ōṭappuraiya eṉṟavai eṉua'' eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟutal kāṇka. (1232)
ōṭāvi = oviyaṉ. ōṭu - ōṭṭam, āṭṭam = oppu.
kuraṅkāṭṭam ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
ōṭṭai - uṭaṉ, otta.
ul - ur - uṟu - uṟaḻ
ur uri - uriñ - uriñcu
ur - ura - urai - urāy.
`ul' aṭiyiṉ aḷḷait tiripu
ul - al. allutal - poruntutal, piṉṉutal - muṭaital.
al - aḷ - aḷai
aḷ - aṇ aṇavu, aṇai
aḷ - aṭṭu. accu (otta poruḷamaikkuṅ karuvi)
aṇ - aṇṭu
attu, am mutaliya coṟkaḷum `aḷ' aṭiyiṉiṉṟu amain tavaiyē, attutal - oṭṭutal, iṇaittal. am - amar. amartal - ottal, poruntutal.
`uḷ' aṭiyaṉ piṉṉait tiripu
uḷ - iḷ. iṇ - iṇar, iṇaṅku, iṇa.
iḷ - (iy) iyai - icai
iḷ - iḻai
iy - ey - ēy.
ḷakara meyyīṟu pala coṟkaḷil yakaramey yīṟākat tiriyum.
e-kā :	toḷ - toy - poḷ - poy - vaḷ - vay. vai - kūrmai iḻaital - urāytal, uḷam poruntutal, oṉṟip paḻakutal eytal - ottal ``tēṉey mārpakam'' (cīvaka 2382) ukarac col, muṉmaik karuttilum uyarccik karuttilum vakarameyc cērkkai peṟuvatu pōṉṟē, oppumaik karuttilum peṟum
uv, uvakkāṇ, uvai - muṉmaiccuṭṭu.
uvvi (talai) uva, uvaṇ, uvaṇai - uyarccik karuttu
(uv) - uva (uvar) - ivar - oppumaik karuttu. ivartal - ottal.
uvamai eṉṉuñ col oppumaik karuttuḷḷa `uva' eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu piṟantatākum uvattal eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku virumputal, piriyamātal, makiḻtal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum uvakai eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku aṉpu, kāmam, iṉpac cuvai (ciruṅkācaracam) makiḻcci eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum, uvappu eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyarkku viruppam, makiḻcci, polivu, eṉṉum poruḷkaḷum. palkalaikkaḻaka akarātiyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṉam ottirā viṭṭāl, viruppamum aṉpum kāmamum iṉpamum makiḻcciyum eṅṅaṉam nikaḻum? ivaṟṟiṟ kellām aṭippaṭai uḷam ottal aṉṟō!
`uvakai' uvappu mutaliya coṟkaḷ pōṉṟē uvamai eṉpatum oru toḻiṟ peyarākum. `uva' pakuti `mai' vikuti. oppu nōkka : iḷa + mai - iḷamai poṟu + mai - poṟumai. iḷattal = meṉmaiyātal.
oruviṉaip pakuti pala vikutikaḷaiyēṟṟup palatoḻiṟ peyarkaḷai ākkutal iyalpātalāl. `uva' eṉṉum pakuti (`tal', `kai', `pu' u, mai, mam, māṉam'' eṉṉum pala vikutikaḷai yēṟṟu muṟaiyē uvattal, uvakai, uvappu. uvavu, uvamai, uvamam, uvamāṉam, eṉṉum palatoḻiṟ peyarkaḷait tōṟṟuvikkum. ivaṟṟuḷ mutal nāṉkum, uvamaip poruḷ iḻantuviṭṭaṉa, allatu avaṟṟai apporuḷil āḷum ilakkiyap pakutikaḷ iṟantu paṭṭaṉa.
`mam' oru toḻiṟ peyarīṟu eṉpatu, urumam, parumam, mutaliya coṟkaḷāl uṇaralām. uruttal veppamātal, urumam veppamāṉa naṇpakal.
``mam'' īṟu ``mai'' yīṟṟu tiripeṉak koḷḷa iṭamuṇṭu.
o. nō : ceyyāmai - ceyyāmal.
ceyyāmai pōṉāṉ, ceyyāmē pōṉāṉ, ceyyāmaṟ pōṉāṉ, eṉṉum mut toṭarum oru poruḷaṉavātal kāṇka.
``maṉṉutiru vaṇṇā
malaiccam pan tāṇ piṟkup
paṉṉu talaic cavaram
paṇṇu vatēṉ - miṉṉiṉ
iḷatta viṭai mātar
ivaṉ kuṭumi paṟṟi
vaḷait tiḻuttuk kuṭṭā
malukku''
eṉṉum kāḷamēkar pāṭalil, kuṭṭāmal eṉpatu kuṭṭāmalukku eṉṟu urupēṟṟu varuvatāl `ceyyāmal' eṉṉum vāypāṭṭu etirmaṟai viṉaiyecca vaṭivam, viṉai eccamāy āḷappaṭum. ceyyāmai eṉṉum etirmaṟait toḻiṟ peyariṉ tiripāyirukkalām eṉṟu uyttuṇarap peṟum.
aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷiṉ makaraviṟuti ṉakaraviṟutiyāvatu perum pāṉmaiyātalāl uvamam eṉṉum peyar uvamaṉ eṉṟu tiriyum.
o. nō :	parumam	-	parumaṉ
	taṭumam	-	taṭumaṉ	}	toḻiṟ peyar
	tiṟam	-	tiṟaṉ
	kaṭam	-	kaṭaṉ	}	piṟa peyar
viḻumam eṉṉum toḻiṟ peyar viḻu + mam eṉṟum, viḻumu + am eṉṟum pirittaṟku iṭantarum. āyiṉ urumam, parumam eṉpaṉa iṭantarā, viḻuttal - ciṟattal, viḻumutal - ciṟattal.
māṉam eṉṉum col toḻiṟ peyarīṟāy varuvatai, aṭaimāṉam, kaṭṭumāṉam, tīrmāṉam, paṭimāṉam, piṭimāṉam, peṟumāṉam, varumāṉam mutaliya toḻiṟ peyarkaḷiṉṟu aṟika.
uvamai eṉṉum peyariṉṟu uvamittal eṉṉum viṉai piṟakkum. orumai eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu orumi - ttal eṉṉum viṉai piṟantiruttal kāṇka. uvamittal oṉṟaṉai oṉṟiṟku oppākak kūṟutal.
iṅṅaṉamē, uvamāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu uvamāṉi - ttal eṉṉum viṉai piṟakkum. tīrmāṉam eṉṉum peyariṉiṉṟu tīrmāṉi - ttal eṉṉum viṉai piṟantiruttal kāṇka. tīrmāṉam muṭipu. tīrmāṉittal muṭipu ceytal uvamāṉittal uvamittal.
uvappu  uṉṉum col muṟkūṟiyavāṟu uyarccip poruḷaiyun tarum.
uva - uvar. ivar - ivartal = uyartal, ēṟutal
uva - uvappu. uyaram, uva = uka - ukappu = uyarvu
uva - uvaṇ - mēliṭam. uvaṇ - uvaṇai - tēvarulakam.
``uyarntataṉ mēṟṟē uḷḷuṅkālai''
eṉṟu tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟuvatāl (1224) uyarnta poruḷai oppākak koḷvatē uvamaiyiṉ aṭippaṭai neṟimuṟai eṉṟu koṇṭu uvamaic colliṟku uyarvupporuḷ. koḷvatu poruntātu. tolkāppiyam uyarntatu eṉṟu kuṟittatu `ciṟantatu' eṉṉum poruḷatu. ciṟappu uyarvu ciṟappu. iḻivu, ciṟappu eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. uyarnta poruḷukku mikavuyarnta poruḷaiyum iḻitē poruḷukku mika iḻinta poruḷaiyum uvamaṅkūṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatē tolkāppiyar karuttu.
e-kā :
``ūraṅ kaṇanīr
uravunīr cērntakkāl
pērum piṟitākit
tīrttamām''
kaṅkaiyāṟṟilum cākkaṭai kalantu kaṅkai nīrām eṉṉum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvamaiyil mēlōrkkuk kaṅkaiyum kīḻōrkkuc cākkaṭaiyum uvamamātal kāṇka. vēṟṟumai kuṟikkum colvēṟṟumai eṉṟē peyar peṟṟāṟ pōl oppumai paṟṟiya aṇiyum oppumai kuṟikkum collā lēyē aḻaikkap peṟutalē muṟaimaiyām.
vaṭamoḻiyil ``upamā'' upamāṉa eṉṉum iruvaṭivukaḷē uḷa. ivai muṟaiyē, uvamai, uvamāṉam, eṉpavaṟṟiṉ tiripukaḷ, uvamam, uvamaṉ, uvami, uvamāṉi eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷum coṟkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyilillai. aṟiyuṅkaruvi allatu aḷavaip peyar `āṉa' eṉṟum kūṟiṉ, ataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum poruḷiṉ peyar `ēya' eṉṟu iṟuvatu vaṭamoḻi marapu.
e-kā:	ñāṉa(m) - ñēya(m)
	aṉumāṉa(m) - aṉumēya(m)
	piramāṇa(m) - piramēya(m)
immuṟaippaṭi upamāṉattiṉāl aṟiyappaṭuvatu upamēya(m) eṉṟāyiṟṟu. itu piṟkāla vaḷarcci māṉam eṉpatu taṉi nilaiyil aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tūya teṉ col. paṭiyai iṉṟum māṉam eṉpar vaṭārkkāṭṭār.
āriyar nāvāmpoḻiṟku varumuṉṉarē. tamiḻilakkaṇam teṉ maturaiyil muḻuvaḷarcciyaṭaintu viṭṭateṉṟum tolkāppiyam ki. mu. 7ām allatu 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēṉum atil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa ilakkaṇam (vaṭacol nīṅkalākat) talaikkaḻakak kālattatu eṉṟum atil āḷappeṟṟuḷḷa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ ellām teṉcollē eṉṟum vaṭamoḻi teṉ moḻiyiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷ āyirak kaṇakkiṉavātalāl, avai vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa aḷavāṉē avaṟṟai āriyam eṉa mayaṅkal kūṭāteṉṟum aṟital vēṇṭum. cilar ōraḷavu vaṭamoḻikaṟṟa aḷavāṉē tammai vaṭamoḻi vallāreṉṟu paṟai cāṟṟik koṇṭu eḷḷaḷavum ārāycciyiṉṟi vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacol eṉa tām mayaṅkuvatōṭamaiyātu māṇavarai yum āciriyaraiyum atikārikaḷaiyum mayakki varukiṉṟaṉar. kalvi vēṟu ārāycci vēṟu. ceṉṉaiyum peṅkaḷūrum pōṉṟa paṉmoḻi vaṭṭāraṅkaḷil cila kūlik kārarum paṉmoḻi pēcukiṉṟaṉar. avarellām moḻinūlaṟiñarō!
tamiḻaic cevvaiyāy aṟitaṟku tamiḻar teṟkiṉiṉṟu vaṭakkup pōntār eṉṉum varalāṟṟuṇmaiyaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṭippaṭai yākum. iḵtillār tamiḻiṉ toṉṉilai aṟital teṉmalai kāṇap poṉmalai celvatē yokkum.
itu kāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl `uvamai' teṉ collē yeṉṟum atu uva eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyaṭip piṟanta toḻiṟ peyareṉṟum ataṉ aṭivēr ukarac col eṉṟum akaṉ kaṇṇum ottaṟ karuttu koṇ ṭuḷḷateṉṟum ataṉāl atu ataṉiṉṟu mōṉai aḷḷai piṉṉait tiripākat tōṉṟiyuḷḷa ottaṟ karuttuc coṟkaḷai yellām tāṅki niṟkum taṉipperum tūṇ eṉṟum aṇikaṭkellāntāyāṉa uvamai yilakkaṇattaiyē aṇiyilakkaṇamākavuṅ kūṟum tolkāppiya uvama iyal vaṭamoḻiyil piṟkālattu virivākat tōṉṟiya aṇinūlkaṭ kellām mūlameṉṟum varalāṟṟu aṭippaṭaiyilē collārāycciyum moḻiyārāycciyum ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ārāycciyiṉṟik kuṟaṭum pētaiyum pōlak koṇṭatu viṭāmai āciriyark kaḻakaṉṟeṉṟum aṟintu koḷka. tolkāppiya ilakkaṇattiṉ toṉmaiyum muṉmaiyum tūymaiyum tāymaiyum ākiya ellā ceytikaḷum eṉ ``tolkāppiyak kaṭṭuraikaḷ'' eṉṉum nūlil virivāka viḷakkappeṟum.
uvamai teṉ collē eṉpataṟku vēṟu cila cāṉṟukaḷumuḷa.
tamiḻil ``uvamai'' eṉṉum collil ``uva'' eṉpatu viṉaippakuti; `mai' eṉpatu vikuti; ākavē, `uva' eṉpatē uyirnāṭiyāṉa uṟuppu.
`mai' vikuti. vantamai. varukiṉṟamai, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟ peyar vikutiyākavum, ciṟumai, poṟumai eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil toḻiṟ paṇpuppeyar vikutiyākavum, naṉmai, tīmai eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa coṟkaḷil paṇpup peyar vikutiyākavum irukkum.
vaṭamoḻiyil `upamā' eṉṉum collil `upa' eṉpatu iṭaic collāṉa muṉṉoṭṭu (upacarkkam. Prefix) eṉṟum `mā' eṉpatu aḷavu kuṟitta colleṉṟum koṇṭu `upamā' eṉpataṟku otta aḷavu oṉṟiṉaik koṇṭu, oṉṟai aḷattal, oppu nōkku, oppumai, uvamai eṉa muṟaiyē poruḷ kūṟappaṭum. ākavē, `mā' eṉpatē ciṟanta uṟuppām. itaṉāl tamiḻil nilaimoḻi allatu piṉ moḻip poruḷ ciṟantum vaṭamoḻiyil varumoḻi allatu muṉmoḻip poruḷ ciṟantum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum teḷivām.
uvamāṉam eṉṉum tamiḻc collil `māṉam' eṉpatu aṭaimāṉam (aṭaivu) paṭimāṉam (paṭivu) eṉpavaṟṟiṟ pōl oru vikuti. upamāṉa eṉṉum vaṭacollil, māṉa eṉpatu aḷavu eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuttap paṭuñ col.
iṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭṭiruppatāl, tamiḻ `uvamai' vēṟu vaṭamoḻi, `upamā' vēṟu eṉṟu karutaṟka. iraṇṭum oṉṟē. vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacollēyeṉṟu kāṭṭutaṟku, atil uḷḷa teṉ coṟkaḷellām poruntap poyttalākavum poruntāp poyttalāka vum poruḷ kūṟuvatum, atuvum iyalākkāl iṭukuṟi (rūṭam) eṉṟu muttiraiyiṭṭu viṭuvatum, vaṭanūlār vaḻakkam. veḷḷaiyāṉa ciṟu tavaca vakai yoṉṟaik kuṟikkum cāmai eṉṉum collaic `ciyāmā' eṉṟu tirittuk karuppāṉatu eṉṟum; naṉṟāy eṇṇeyil ventapiṉ tiṉṉum vaṭai vakai yoṉṟaik kuṟikkum āmaivaṭai eṉṉum collai `āmavaṭa' eṉattirittu `naṉṟāka vēkātatu' eṉṟum; mukam eṉpataṉ kaṭaip pōliyāṉa mukaṉ eṉṉum collai `mu. kaṉ' eṉappirittu, tōṇṭap peṟṟa kiṭaṅku pōṉṟa vāyaiyuṭaiya uṟuppu eṉṟum, vaṭamuṉaiyāy tōṉṟum oruvakai neruppaik kuṟikkum vaṭavai eṉṉum collaip `paṭapā' eṉṟu tirittu. ataṉoṭumukam eṉpataic cērttup `paṭapāmukam' eṉa ākki, ``peṭṭaikkutiraimukam pōṉṟatu eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvār vēṟu eṉtāṉ collār!
tolkāppiyattilum toṉṉūlkaḷilum uvamai aṇiyilakkaṇa muṟaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭāmal poruḷilakkaṇa muṟaiyilēyē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tōḻi, talaivaṉ talaiviyoṭu, ciṟappākat talaivaṉoṭu uraiyāṭum pōtu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam ēṉaiyuvamam eṉṉum iruvakai yuvamai kaḷaiyum āḷuvatu marapu. ivaṟṟai viḷakka eḻuntatē uvamayiyal, piṟkālattil vaṭanūlār aṇiyilakkaṇam vakutta pōtu tamiḻilakkaṇa uvamayiyalai payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭatuṭaṉ uvamai eṉṉum peyaraiyum `upamā' eṉat tirittuk koṇṭaṉar. tolkāppiyam pāṇiṉīyattiṟku muntiyateṉṉum uṇmai oppuk koḷḷappaṭāviṭiṉum, tolkāppiyam kaṭaikkaḻakat toṭakkatteḻunta vaḻi nūlātalāl ataṟku muntiya akattiyam mutaliya nūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇaṅkaṭku mutaṉūl eṉpatai evarum maṟukka muṭiyātu. mēlum aintiram pāṇiṉīyam mutaliya vaṭamoḻi viyākaraṇaṅkaḷ ellām naṉṉūl pōl eḻuttum collum ākiya iraṇṭē kūṟuvaṉaveṉṟum tolkāppiyam mutaliya toṉṉūlkaḷ yāvum eḻuttuc coṟ poruḷ mūṉṟaiyuṅ kūṟum piṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟum vēṟu pāṭaṟital vēṇṭum. vaṭamoḻiyil, yāppilakkaṇam cantacu allatu cantōpiciti eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum, aṇiyilakkaṇam alaṅkāram eṉṉum nūlkaḷilum vēṟākak kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻilō, yāppum aṇiyum poruḷilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷ. avai taṉi nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaviṭattum, poruḷ ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷākavē eṉṟuṅ koḷḷap peṟum. uvamai aṇikaṭkellām tāyātalāl, aṇiyilakkaṇam uvamayiyal aṭakkamām.
tolkāppiyar uvamaviyalil nālvakai ēṉaiyuvamaṅkaḷum aivakai uḷḷuṟai uvamaṅkaḷum kūṟappeṟṟuḷḷaṉa; uvamai uṟuppukaḷ māṇavarkku eḷitāy viḷaṅkuvaṉavātalāl kūṟappeṟavillai. āyiṉ, eccaviyalil uvamattokai kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāl ataṉ maṟutalaiyākiya uvama viriyum kūṟāmal kūṟappaṭṭatākak koḷḷap peṟum.
uvamam, uvamavurupu potut taṉmai, poruḷ eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamai uṟuppukkaḷām. innāṉkum amaivatu viriyuvamai; uvama urupu allatu uvamavurupum kotut taṉmaiyum kuṟaivatu tokaiyuvamai. vaṭamoḻiyil innāṉkaiyum muṟaiyē, upamāṉa, upama vācaka, cātāraṇa tarma, upamēya eṉṟum; viriyuvamattaip pūrṇōpamā eṉṟum; tokai yuvamattai `luptōpamā' eṉṟum aḻaippar. ivaiyellām piṟkālattu vaḷarcciyum moḻi peyarppumākum.
itu kāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl `uvamai' teṉcollē eṉṟum uvamayiyal teṉ moḻiyilakkaṇa kūṟē eṉṟum tērntu teḷika. tāḻvuṇarcci nīṅkuka! talai nimirka!
13
tiraviṭam teṉ colliṉ tiripē
aṅkam, vaṅkam, kaliṅkam, tiraviṭam - `am' īṟupeṟṟaṉa. nāṭukaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ - kālṭuvel kāṭṭiya mēṟkōḷum tāyumāṉavar pāṭiya pāṭalum tamiḻ tirinta vaḻiyum vaḻaṅkiya muṟaiyum.
tiraviṭam allatu tirāviṭam eṉṉum col em moḻiyatu eṉpatu iṉṟum palarkkut teriyātiruppatāṉum, aṇmaiyil atupaṟṟi ōr aiyaviṉā teṉṟalil tōṉṟiyatāṉum ammayakkai aṟavē aṟuttaṟku eḻuntatik kaṭṭurai.
nāṭṭup peyarkaḷum moḻip peyarkaḷum paṇṭaik kālattu `am' īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpatu,
``aṅkam vaṅkam kaliṅkam kaucikam
cintu cōṉakam tiraviṭam ciṅkaḷam
makatam kōcalam marāṭam koṅkaṇam
tuḷuvam cāvakam cīṉam kāmpōcam
paruṇampap parameṉap patiṉeṇpāṭai''
eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpāvum,
ciṅkaḷam cōṉakam cāvakam cīṉam tuḷukkuṭakam
koṅkaṇam kaṉṉaṭam
kollam teluṅkam kaliṅkam vaṅkam
kaṅkam makatam kaṭāram kavuṭam kaṭuṅkucalam
taṅkum pukaḻttamiḻ cūḻpati ṉēḻpuvi tāmivaiyē
eṉṉum paḻañ ceyyuḷum uṇarttum. immuṟai paṟṟit tamiḻum tamiḻam eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itaṉālēyē, īṟukeṭṭa makara vīṟṟup puṇarcciyāka tamiḻap piḷḷai, tamiḻanākaṉ, tamiḻavaṉavaṉ mutaliya puṇar moḻikaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. naṉṉūlār ivaṟṟait tamiḻ eṉṉum nilaimoḻi akarac cāriyai peṟṟup puṇarntateṉṟum, tolkāppiyar ammoḻi akkuc cāriyai peṟṟup puṇarntateṉṟum, kūṟiṉar. ivar kūṟṟiṉpaṭi, koṅkavaṇṇāṉ eṉpataik koṅku eṉṉum nilaimoḻi akara allatu akkuc cāriyai peṟṟup puṇarntatākavum, tuḷuva vēḷāḷaṉ eṉpatait tuḷu eṉṉum nilaimoḻi avvāṟu puṇarntatākavum, koḷḷavēṇṭi varumātaliṉ, atu poruntāteṉa viṭukka. tamiḻ eṉṉum col mutalāvatu vaṭamoḻiyil tirinta vaṭivam tramiḷam eṉpatē. ḻakaram vaṭa moḻiyiliṉmaiyālum, uyirmeym mutalai meymmutalākki rakarattai vaḻic cerukal am moḻikkiyalpātalāṉum, accol ammoḻiyil avvaṭivai aṭainta teṉka. tiramiḷam eṉpatu tamiḻ eṉat tiritalumatu eṉṟu pirayōka vivēka nūlār (pak. 4) talaikīḻākak kūṟiya kūṟṟil, avvaṭivē āḷap peṟutal kāṇka.
kālṭuvel. purāṇaṅkaḷiṉ paḻaiya malaiyāḷamoḻi peyarppu kaḷilellām ivvaṭivē peruvaḻakkāyiruppatākak kuṇṭarṭṭuk kūṟuvatākavum avar eṭutturaippar. tiramiḷam eṉṉum colliṟku (1) pañca tiraviṭa tēcaṅkaḷ, (2) tamiḻ eṉa iruporuḷ kūṟum ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻakarātiyum.
tiramiḷam eṉpatu piṉpu tramiṭam eṉat tirintatu. timiṭam eṉpatum ciṟitu kālattiṉ piṉ traviṭam eṉat tiriyalāyiṟṟu. iv viṟuti vaṭivattiṉ nīṭciyē trāviṭam eṉpatu. itu tamiḻil tirāviṭam eṉṟākum.
tramiḷam, tramiṭam, traviṭam eṉṉuñ rakarañ cerukiya vaṭivu kaḷellām, vaṭamoḻi vaḻakkēyaṉṟip piṟamoḻi allatu piṟanāṭṭu vaḻakkalla. piṟamoḻikaḷellām tamiḻiṟ pōl rakaramaṟṟa vaṭivē vaḻaṅkum. ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāviṟku vanta iveṉ ticāṅ (Hwen Thsang) eṉṉum cīṉa vaḻippōkkar kuṟippil, cimolo (Tchimo - lo) eṉṉum vaṭivam uḷḷatu. itait timala (Dimala) allatu timara (Dimara) eṉṟum paṭikkalām eṉpar kālṭuvel. pālimoḻiyiluḷḷa makāvamicam (mahavanso) eṉṉum ilaṅkai varalāṟṟil tamilo' (Damilo) eṉṉum vaṭivam uḷḷatu. airōppiyar potuvākat tamul (Tamul) eṉṟaṉar. avaruḷ tēṉiya viṭaiyūḻiyar - (Danish missionaries) maṭṭum tamulikka moḻi (Lingua Darmulia) eṉa aḻaittaṉar. āṅkilattil `tamiḻ (Tamil) eṉṉum vaṭivam vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tamiḻakam eṉṉum peyar, mēṉāṭṭup paḻaiya tēcappaṭaṅkaḷilum ñālanūṟ cuvaṭikaḷilum `tamirikke' (Damirice) eṉṟum, timirikka (Dimirica) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muḻukippōṉa kumarikkaṇṭattil ki. mu. (ēṟattāḻa) aimpatiṉāyiram āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ muḷaitteḻuntatum, tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkellān tāyumāṉa paḻantamiḻ oru kālattil paṉimalai (imayam) varai paravi yiruntatālum, ki. mu. 1000 āṇṭukaṭkup piṉṉarē teluṅku mutaliya tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ ataṉiṉṟu kiḷaittatālum, avaṟṟuḷḷum orumoḻiyilum ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ ilakkiyan tōṉṟāmaiyālum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻaiyum tamiḻiṉattaiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭaiyumē mutaṟkālattil kuṟittu niṉṟatu. tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ kiḷaitta piṉpum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum col tamiḻait taṉippaṭak kuṟikkum vaḻakkum kālṭuvel kālam varai toṭarntu vantiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
piḷṉai lōkāriya cīyar (500 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ)
``neñcuk kiṟāḷkaṭi tīpam
aṭaṅkā neṭum piṟavi
nañcukku nalla amutam.
tamiḻa naṉṉūṟ ṟuṟaikaḷ
añcuk kilakkiyam āraṇa
cāram paraca mayap
pañcuk kaṉaliṉ poṟipara
kālaṉ paṉu valkaḷē''
eṉṟa periya tirumoḻic ciṟappup pāyirattil, tamiḻa naṉṉūṟṟuṟai kaḷañcukku eṉpataṉ poruḷai viḷakkumiṭattu, tiraviṭa cātiram, eḻuttu col poruḷ yāppu alaṅkāram eṉkiṟa vilakkaṇamāṉa pañcala kṣaṇat tōṭē kūṭiyiṟē niṟpatu eṉa varaintirukkiṉṟār.
18 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil vāḻntirunta tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ pāṭiya kallāta pērkaḷē nallavarkaḷ eṉṉum ceyyuḷil,
``vaṭamoḻiyilē
vallāṉ oruttaṉ vara
vuntirā viṭattilē
vantatā vivakarippēṉ
valla tamiḻaṟiñar variṉ
aṅṅaṉē vaṭa moḻiyil
vacaṉaṅkaḷ ciṟitu pukalvēṉ''
eṉṉum pakutiyil, tiraviṭam tamiḻ eṉṉum iru collum oru moḻi yaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. āḻvārkaḷ aruḷic ceyta nālāyirat teyvap paṉuval, tamiḻ vētam, tiraviṭavētam, tiraviṭap pirapantam eṉṉum peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
pākavata purāṇattil, cattiya virataṉ eṉṉum peyarāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum oru tamiḻaracaṉ tiraviṭapati eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. tiraviṭam eṉṉum collukku, tamiḻnāṭu eṉṉum poruḷum akarātikaḷiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
āriyar intiyāviṟ kuṭipukuntu vaṭa intiyā muḻutum paraviya piṉ kumarilapaṭṭar kālam (7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) varai teṉṉāṭum, teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷiṉaṅkaḷum avai pēciya moḻikaḷum, tamiḻiṉ talaimaipaṟṟi, tiraviṭam eṉṟē aḻaikkappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
maṉu, tam (āriya) aṟa nūlil (10 : 43,44) āriyarallāta palvēṟu iṉat tāraik kuṟikkumiṭattu tamiḻaraiyum teluṅkar mutaliya tirāviṭaraiyum vēṟupaṭuttātu avarellāraiyum oruṅkē taḻuvumāṟu - tirāviṭar eṉṉum collaiyē āṇṭirukkiṉṟār. vintiya malaikkut teṟkil vantu kuṭiyēṟiya pirāmaṇarum, tirāviṭap pirāmaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. itu nāṭu paṟṟiyatēyaṉṟi iṉampaṟṟiyataṉṟu. malēyāt tamiḻar, teṉṉāppirikkat tamiḻar eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. immuṟai paṟṟiyē, tēvāra mūvaruḷ oruvarāṉa tiruñāṉacampantarum tirāviṭa cicu eṉappaṭṭār. vaṭa nāṭṭu moḻi nūlaṟiñar, mutaṟkaṇ, (āriya) vēta kālattu intiya vaṭṭāra moḻikaḷaic camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyuṇmai paṟṟip pirākirutam eṉa aḻaittaṉar. avaṟṟil aḷaviṟanta āriyar coṟkaḷ kalantu citainta vaṭiviṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu paṟṟi, piṟ kālattār ammoḻikaḷai vaṭamoḻi (camaṟkiruta) vaḻiyiṉavākap piṟaḻavuṇarntaṉar. itē kāraṇam paṟṟi, paḻañcēra nāṭṭut tamiḻt tiripākiya malaiyāḷattai vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟu vantatāka iṉṟu cilar kūṟuvatu kāṇka.
ki. mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉarākak karutappaṭum kāttiyāyaṉar, paicāci, cauracēṉi, mākati, makārāṭṭiri eṉap pirākiruta moḻikaḷai nāṉkākavē koṇṭaṉar. tirāviṭattai avar koḷḷātatu aṟiyāmaiyō puṟakkaṇippō aṟikilam.
orukāl makārāṭṭiriyil atai aṭakkiṉar pōlum. piṟkālat tāciriyar cilar tirāviṭi eṉpatu oru ciṟutarap pirākirutam eṉṟaṉar. āyiṉ 19ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta pāpu rācēntiralāl mittirā eṉṉum vaṅkāḷar, atai cūra cēṉiyōṭotta perum pirākirutam eṉak kūṟiṉar.
tamiḻiṉiṉṟu mutalāvatu pirinta tiraviṭa moḻi. teluṅkākum. atu vaṭa ticaiyil tōṉṟiyamai paṟṟi vaṭuku eṉappaṭṭatu. kumarilapaṭṭar kālattil teluṅku taṉittuk kūṟappaṭumaḷavu vaḷarcciyaṭaintuviṭṭataṉāl, avar atuvarai tirāviṭam eṉṉum potup peyarālēyē vaḻaṅkiya centamiḻaiyum koṭun tamiḻkaḷaiyum, teluṅku tamiḻ moḻi eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa āntira tirāviṭa `pācai' eṉṟaṉar. teluṅkiṟkup piṉ tamiḻiṉiṉṟu pirinta perumoḻi kaṉṉaṭam ataṉāl
vaṭakalai teṉkalai vaṭuku kaṉṉaṭam eṉak kamparāmāyaṇac ciṟappuppāyirac ceyyuḷoṉṟil kaṉṉaṭamum cērkkap peṟṟatu. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkuppiṉ, malaiyāḷam, tuḷu, kuṭakam mutaliya piṟa koṭun tamiḻkaḷum pirintu pōyiṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil kālṭuvel tiraviṭa moḻikaḷ patiṉmūṉṟeṉak kaṇakkiṭṭār. iṉṟō avai pattoṉpa teṉap parō, emaṉō eṉṉum iru mēlai moḻi nūl vallārāl kaṇak kiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru kālattil intiyā muḻutum tiraviṭam paravi yiruntateṉpataṟku - iṉṟum peluc cittāṉattil pirākuvīyum, vaṅkāḷat til irācamakālum vaḻaṅki varuvatē pōtiya cāṉṟām. āriyar vantu vaṭa intiyā muḻutum paraviyapiṉ, vintiya malaikkut teṟkiluḷḷa moḻika ḷellām tiraviṭam eṉṉuṅ koḷkaiyiruntatu. ataṉālēyē, teluṅku, karunāṭakam, marātti, kūrccaram, (kucarātti) tirāviṭam eṉṉum aintum pañca tirāviṭam eṉṟu vaṭa nūlkaḷum kūṟiṉa. ippaṭṭiyil tirāviṭam eṉṉum peyar tamiḻai maṭṭuṅ kuṟittal kavaṉikkattakkatu. tirāviṭa(m) eṉpatu cila vaṭa nūlkaḷil tirāvira(m) eṉṟum tirintatu. ṭakaram rakaramāṉatu pōli. itukāruṅkūṟiyavaṟṟāl, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻaiyē taṉippaṭavum talaimaiyākavum kuṟittu vanta tiraviṭam eṉṉum col, tamiḻam eṉpataṉ tiripē eṉpatu, varalāṟṟuṇarcci yuṭaiyārkkellām teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkutal tiṇṇam. āyiṉum, āṉaikkum aṭicaṟukkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻikkēṟpa, kālṭuvel tiraviṭam eṉṉum collē muṟaiyē, (traviṭam - tramiṭam - tramiḷa tamiḻ) eṉat tirintateṉṟu talaikīḻākak koṇṭār. āyiṉ, kiraiyarcaṉ atait tirutti uṇmaiyaik kūṟiyatu makiḻccikkuriyatu. eṉiṉum, iṉṟum cilar mayaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇam varalāṟṟaṟiviṉmaiyē. āriyar intiyāviṟ kāl vaittu ki. mu. 3000 āṇṭellaiyeṉṟum, tamiḻ muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭaintu muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiya talaik kaḻakak kālam ki. mu. 10,000 āṇṭellaiyeṉṟum, aṟiyiṉ, oruvarum aiyuṟār eṉpatu tēṟṟam. vaṭamoḻiyil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷiruntum avaṟṟai yellām vaṭacolleṉak kāṭṭuvataiyē vaṭamoḻi yakarātikaḷellām kōṭpāṭākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉum mayakku pulavaruḷḷattilu miruntatiṉāl kampar atait ``tēvapāṭai'' eṉṟār. tolkāppiyac collatikāravuraiyil (401) tamiḻc col vaṭapāṭaikkaṭ cellāmaiyāṉum, vaṭacol ellāt tēyattiṟkum potuvātalāṉum eṉavuraittār cēṉāvaraiyar. ittakaiya nilaikaḷ, vaṭavar tiraviṭam eṉṉum coṟkup poruntap poyttalākac cila poruṭ kāraṇam kāṭṭat tūṇṭiṉa. tru eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vērturattutaṟ poruḷ taruvateṉṟum, āriyarāl teṟkē turattap paṭṭavar tiraviṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar oru cārār. tiraviṭam eṉpatu teṟku eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvateṉṟum, teṉkōṭi mākāṇattār tiraviṭar eṉappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum kūṟuvar maṟṟoru cārār. tiraviṭam teṟkēyiruppatiṉālēyē, tiraviṭam eṉṉum coṟkut teṟkeṉṉum vaḻip poruḷ tōṉṟiṟṟu. civañāṉa muṉivar uḷḷataik koṇṭu nallataip paṇṇum muṟaiyil, turattutal allatu oṭṭutal eṉṉum vērpporuḷai oppuk koṇṭu, tīviṉai yakaṟṟum aṟa nūlkaḷ tamiḻiṟ ciṟantiruppatu paṟṟi, tam kāñcippurāṇattil,
evviṉaiyum opputalāl
tirāviṭam eṉṟiyal pāṉaṭa'' eṉṟu pāṭiṉār.
tru eṉṉum vaṭamoḻi vēr `tura' eṉṉum tamiḻ vēriṉ tiripē. ittamiḻ vēr mēlaiyāriyattil tiyūttāṉiyam eṉṉum kiḷaiyilum ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cerumaṉ : Treiben - paṇṭai āṅkilam Drifen.
āṅkilam : Drive,
turattal eṉpatu ōṭṭutal.
ākavē, vaṭamoḻiyār kūṟum iru poruḷum, tiraviṭam eṉṉum collai vaṭacollākak kāṭṭa iyalāmai kāṇka.
iṉi, oru cila tamiḻar, tiru + iṭam = tiruviṭam - tiraviṭam - tirāviṭam eṉa vantatākak kāṭṭuvar. ivvaralāṟu uttikkup poruntāmaiyōṭu cāṉṟum aṟṟatu. oru cār iḷampulavar, tiru + iṭam = tirāviṭam eṉap puṇarttu,
